投出宇宙曲线球：旋转次级体的极端质量比旋进中的环境效应

Pitching Cosmic Curveballs: Environmental Effects on Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals with Spinning Secondaries

Leif Lui, Lisa V. Drummond, Alejandro Torres-Orjuela · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：类似于旋转曲线球的空气动力学偏转，极端质量比旋进（EMRI）中的旋转次级体在穿越气态环境时，除标准阻力外，还会经历马格努斯力和升力。我们提出了第一个将这些特定的自旋耦合环境效应（EEs）纳入EMRI演化的框架。在空间引力波（GW）探测器长达数年的观测窗口内，这些相互作用会在信号上留下独特且可区分的失谐特征。关键在于，Fisher矩阵分析表明，气体阻力打破了次级体自旋大小与倾角之间的基本真空投影简并，从而加强了参数约束。因此，考虑环境效应不仅是建模的必要条件，还可能成为增强次级体内禀自旋可探测性的有力工具，并可作为探测超大质量黑洞周围吸积流的新途径。

English: Much like the aerodynamic deflection of a spinning curveball, a rotating secondary in an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) experiences Magnus and lift forces, in addition to the standard drag force, when traversing a gaseous environment. We present the first framework that incorporates these specific spin-coupled environmental effects (EEs) into the evolution of EMRI. Over the multi-year observation windows of space-based gravitational-wave (GW) detectors, these interactions imprint a unique, distinguishable dephasing signature on the signal. Crucially, a Fisher matrix analysis reveals that gas drag breaks the fundamental vacuum-projection degeneracy between the secondary's spin magnitude and inclination, thereby tightening parameter constraints. Thus, accounting for EEs is not merely a modeling necessity, but could potentially be a powerful tool for enhancing the detectability of the secondary's intrinsic spin, and could serve as a novel probe of accretion flows harboring massive black holes.

一句话导读：首个将旋转次级体受气体环境导致的马格努斯/升力效应纳入EMRI演化的框架，可打破自旋参数简并并增强引力波探测能力。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典引力中的EMRI与引力波信号，直接属于用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”范畴。

慢收缩宇宙学中的因果视界、测地完备性与稳定性

Causal Horizons, Geodesic Completeness and Stability in Slow Contraction Cosmology

Mariam Khaldieh, Anna I. Rosenzweig, Paul J. Steinhardt · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们证明，具有半无限慢收缩（ekpyrotic）阶段的宇宙学模型具备一系列性质组合，能够解决标准膨胀、收缩 de Sitter 阶段中面临的若干基本宇宙学问题。特别地，慢收缩允许一个稳定过去吸引子渐近于闵可夫斯基时空，以及一个具有可忽略 Weyl 曲率（因此引力熵可忽略）的稳定平坦、均匀、各向同性未来吸引子。在反弹宇宙学中，这一收缩吸引子由一个光滑、非奇异的反弹所终止，将收缩结束时的吸引子性质转化为随后膨胀阶段的初始条件。包含慢收缩阶段的宇宙学没有粒子视界，因此避免了因果视界问题。过去的闵可夫斯基吸引子还在所有波长上产生真空涨落的初始谱。此外，由于沿过去定向测地线的平均膨胀率非正，包含半无限慢收缩阶段的模型也规避了 Borde、Guth 和 Vilenkin 定理，并且在过去方向上是测地完备的。相形之下，收缩的 de Sitter 空间具有有限粒子视界，并且在存在标量场、物质或辐射时变得不稳定。

English: We show that cosmological models with a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction (ekpyrosis) possess a combination of properties that can address several fundamental problems in cosmology, otherwise faced in contracting de Sitter phases or standard big bang expansion. In particular, slow contraction admits a stable past attractor asymptoting to Minkowski space, as well as a stable, flat, homogeneous, and isotropic future attractor with negligible Weyl curvature (and, therefore, negligible gravitational entropy). In bouncing cosmologies, this contracting attractor is terminated by a smooth, non-singular bounce that transforms the attractor properties at the end of contraction into the initial conditions for the subsequent expanding phase. Cosmologies incorporating a slow contraction phase have no particle horizon and therefore avoid the causal horizon problem. The past Minkowski attractor also generates an initial spectrum of vacuum-like quantum fluctuations on all wavelengths. Moreover, because the averaged expansion rate along past-directed geodesics is non-positive, models incorporating a semi-infinite phase of slow contraction also evade the Borde,Guth and Vilenkin theorem and are past geodesically complete. By contrast, contracting de Sitter space possesses a finite particle horizon and becomes unstable in the presence of scalar fields, matter, or radiation.

一句话导读：该文论证慢收缩宇宙学具有稳定的闵可夫斯基过去吸引子、无粒子视界、过去测地完备性等特征，并通过非奇异反弹设定膨胀初始条件；相比而言，收缩 de Sitter 具有有限视界且不稳定。

相关度：low — 论文研究的是经典宇宙学中的慢收缩模型，与用户侧重的全息超导、p波与多分量序参量、QNM与非线性演化等强关联/全息方向无直接交集，仅与经典引力/广义相对论大背景相关。

修正的black bounce致密天体的热力学与辐射预言

Corrected thermodynamics and radiation predictions of modified black bounce compact objects

Shokhzod Jumaniyozov, Javlon Rayimbaev, Yassine Sekhmani, Satimbay Palvanov, Olmos Tursunboyev, Dilshod Karshiev · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了将Simpson-Visser(SV)正则化应用于Schwarzschild修改引力黑洞所得到的正则致密天体的热力学和辐射性质。所得SV-MOG时空的lapse函数同时涉及MOG耦合参数alpha和black-bounce参数l，它随参数l平滑插值于正则黑洞、单向虫洞和可穿越虫洞之间。我们推导了黑洞分支的Hawking温度和热容，通过CV的符号变化识别出二级相变；对于没有因果视界因而没有真正Hawking辐射的无视界虫洞分支，我们改为从不稳定光子球的Lyapunov指数构造物理上定义良好的有效温度。通过由参数beta1、beta2参数化的对数项纳入对熵的量子引力修正，我们讨论了在圈量子引力和弦理论中理论上偏好范围，并显示相对于Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的偏差在小视界半径处变得显著。进一步的线性标量扰动分析表明，有效径向势在整个黑洞外部以及虫洞喉部保持非负，说明与从热容推断的热力学稳定性无关，系统对单极扰动是稳定的。

English: We study the thermodynamic properties and radiation characteristics of a regular compact object obtained by applying the Simpson-Visser (SV) regularisation to the Schwarzschild modified gravity black hole. The resulting SV-MOG spacetime, whose lapse function involves both the MOG coupling parameter alpha and the black-bounce parameter l, smoothly interpolates between a regular black hole, a one-way wormhole, and a traversable wormhole depending on the parameter l. We derive the Hawking temperature and heat capacity for the black hole branch, identifying second-order phase transitions signaled by sign changes in CV, and, for the horizonless wormhole branch where no causal horizon and hence no genuine Hawking radiation exists we instead construct a physically well-defined effective temperature from the Lyapunov exponent of the unstable photon sphere. Quantum gravitational corrections to the entropy are incorporated via logarithmic terms parameterized by coefficients beta1, beta2, whose theoretically preferred ranges in loop quantum gravity and string theory we discuss, and we show that deviations from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law become significant at small horizon radii. A linear scalar-perturbation analysis further shows that the effective radial potential remains non-negative throughout the black-hole exterior and across the wormhole throat, indicating stability against monopole perturbations independently of the thermodynamic stability inferred from the heat capacity.

一句话导读：研究修改的black bounce致密天体的热力学、Hawking/有效温度、热容相变和量子引力修正熵，并检验标量扰动稳定性。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦经典/修改引力下的黑洞与虫洞热力学和辐射，不涉及AdS/CFT等全息方向；仅匹配经典引力/广义相对论支线，故为中等相关。

月球激光干涉仪天线（LILA）选址研究

Site selection for the Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA)

James Trippe, Ronald Polidan, Teviet Creighton, Philippe Lognonn\'e, Mark Panning, Volker Quetschke, Kris Izquierdo, Brett Shapiro, Karan Jani · astro-ph.IM, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：地球的月球为探测天体物理引力波，特别是分赫兹频段的引力波，提供了独特而有利的环境。激光干涉仪月球天线（LILA）项目计划利用月球的低地震噪声在月球表面进行引力波测量。研究考虑两种任务概念：1公里长度的概念验证型初始LILA（iLILA）和臂长至少40公里的完整LILA天文台。月球的朝向变化与轨道运动提供了源定位所需的时间相关调制，因此探测器响应并不限定唯一月面区域。然而，实际因素（尤其是视线条件）限制了部署位置。通过从粗到细的现有月球数据集搜索，识别并排序了候选网格位置。在4050个全球网格种子中，有434个iLILA种子和71个天文台外心通过了两阶段搜索。结果表明，适合引力波探测的月面地点自然大量存在。

English: The Earth's Moon presents a uniquely advantageous environment for detecting astrophysical gravitational waves (GWs), particularly in the decihertz regime. The Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA) project plans to perform GW measurements on the lunar surface, using the Moon's seismic quietness to access this band. Two mission concepts are considered: the proof-of-concept 1 km initial LILA (iLILA) and the full LILA Observatory, whose equilateral arms are at least 40 km long. The Moon's changing orientation and orbital motion provide the time-dependent source modulation needed for sky localization, so the detector response does not impose a unique lunar region. Practical considerations, most critically line of sight (LOS), nevertheless constrain deployment. A coarse-to-fine search of existing lunar datasets identifies and ranks candidate grid locations. Of 4,050 global grid seeds, 434 iLILA seeds and 71 Observatory circumcenters pass both search stages. These results demonstrate that sites suitable for GW detection naturally exist in large numbers on the lunar surface.

一句话导读：评估月球引力波探测器LILA的候选选址，发现月球表面存在大量适合探测的地点。

相关度：low — 论文属于引力波探测实验选址研究，与理论全息或量子引力无关，仅与经典引力/广义相对论中的引力波方向有间接关联。

基于原子干涉仪的地面引力波探测

Terrestrial Gravitational Wave Detection with Atom Interferometers

Michael Werner, Ashkan Alibabaei, Naceur Gaaloul · quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：原子干涉仪（AIFs）是高精度惯性传感器，被认为是在中频段探测引力波（GW）和某些暗物质（DM）候选者的有前景仪器。虽然最初提出的基于AIF的引力波探测是空间任务，基线跨度达数千公里，但近期发展表明，基线至少100米的地面装置也可能探测到引力波。在这项工作中，我们解析地推导了地面探测器的引力波相位响应公式，发现其中比现有文献多出一项，并通过数值计算验证了我们的结果。基于这一处理方法，我们分析了地球约束AIF实验的最优几何参数。随后，我们使用一个开源Python算法对这些优化后的干涉仪的引力波探测方案进行了数值模拟。这些方案的数值模拟此前尚未公开，而对于精确模拟噪声和非平凡的引力背景至关重要。

English: Atom interferometers (AIFs) are highly precise inertial sensors and are considered promising instruments for the detection of gravitational waves (GWs) and certain dark matter (DM) candidates in the mid-frequency band. While GW detection with AIFs was initially proposed for space-based experiments with baselines spanning thousands of kilometers, recent developments suggest that terrestrial setups with baselines of at least 100 meters may also be capable of detecting GWs. In this work, we analytically derive the GW phase response formula of a ground detector, find an additional term compared to the existing literature and check it our findings numerically. Based on this treatment, we analyze the optimal geometric parameters for earth-bound AIF experiments. Subsequently, we numerically simulate GW detection schemes for these optimized interferometers using an open-source Python algorithm. Numerical simulations of these schemes have are not available to the community, yet crucial for the accurate modeling of noise and non-trivial gravitational backgrounds.

一句话导读：解析推导地面原子干涉仪引力波相位响应公式，发现新项，并优化几何参数及数值模拟。

相关度：low — 该论文聚焦于经典引力波探测技术，与全息对偶或量子引力等理论无关，仅与广义相对论的实验检验间接相关。

体拓扑序与边界混合态序之间的全息对偶

Holographic duality between bulk topological order and boundary mixed-state order

Tsung-Cheng Lu, Yu-Jie Liu, Sarang Gopalakrishnan, Yizhi You · quant-ph, cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.str-el · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：引入一个全息框架来分析具有强对称性的重复量子信道的稳态。利用信道-态对偶，证明 d 维量子信道的稳态被全息映射到一个由顺序幺正电路生成的 (d+1) 维波函数的边界约化密度矩阵。从这一视角，稳态中的强到弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）源于高一个维度的拓扑序边界上的任意子凝聚，与 SWSSB 关联的条件互信息则继承自体拓扑纠缠熵。通过等距张量网络态（isoTNS）将信道的时间演化与高维 isoTNS 的转移矩阵等同，使该对偶具体化，并进一步构造了连续可调的量子信道，展示稳态中不同的混合态相和相变。

English: We introduce a holographic framework for analyzing the steady states of repeated quantum channels with strong symmetries. Using channel-state duality, we show that the steady state of a $d$-dimensional quantum channel is holographically mapped to the boundary reduced density matrix of a $(d+1)$-dimensional wavefunction generated by a sequential unitary circuit. From this perspective, strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) in the steady state arises from the anyon condensation on the boundary of a topological order in one higher dimension. The conditional mutual information (CMI) associated with SWSSB is then inherited from the bulk topological entanglement entropy. We make this duality explicit using isometric tensor network states (isoTNS) by identifying the channel's time evolution with the transfer matrix of a higher-dimensional isoTNS. Built on isoTNS, we further construct continuously tunable quantum channels that exhibit distinct mixed-state phases and transitions in the steady states.

一句话导读：通过量子信道稳态与高维体拓扑序边界态的对应，揭示 SWSSB 源于任意子凝聚，并利用等距张量网络构造了可调混合态相变模型。

相关度：low — 论文研究量子信道与拓扑序的体-边界全息对偶，属于量子信息/张量网络全息，而非传统 AdS/CFT 引力全息或用户关注的全息超导、经典引力等方向，与重点标签无交集。

奇异和粲矢量介子自旋排列的介质效应

Medium effect on spin alignment of strange and charm vector mesons

Ruixiang Chen, Hiwa A. Ahmed, Yidian Chen, Mei Huang · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：理解相对论性重离子碰撞中矢量介子的自旋排列需要对其自旋依赖的介质内性质进行非微扰描述。我们在一个统一的四味软墙全息框架内研究该问题，该框架结合了有限温度、重子化学势和角速度下的各向异性爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-膨胀子背景。自旋排列通过瞬时冻结出射方案，由自旋分辨的矢量流谱函数的介质诱导劈裂确定。我们系统地研究了奇异和粲矢量介子 K^*、φ、D^*、D_s^* 和 J/ψ，以及它们的自旋排列对横动量、快度、温度、重子化学势和角速度的依赖。我们发现重粲矢量介子 D^*、D_s^* 和 J/ψ 在低横动量下表现为 ρ_00 > 1/3，而轻奇异矢量介子 K^* 和 φ 则表现出相反的低动量行为和角分布，ρ_00 < 1/3。我们将这种味道依赖的分离归因于真空质量壳相对于热移动的纵、横谱峰位置的不同。结果定性地重现了低和中等横动量下观察到的若干趋势。自旋排列对重子化学势不敏感，仅受角速度弱影响。这些结果为跨味道扇区的矢量介子自旋排列建立了一个平衡全息基线，并有助于圈定额外机制（如非平衡演化、涨落和硬产生）变得重要的区域。

English: Understanding the spin alignment of vector mesons in relativistic heavy-ion collisions requires a nonperturbative description of their spin-dependent in-medium properties. We investigate this problem within a unified four-flavor soft-wall holographic framework that combines an anisotropic Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton background at finite temperature, baryon chemical potential, and angular velocity. Spin alignment is determined from the medium-induced splitting of the spin-resolved vector-current spectral functions through an instantaneous freeze-out prescription. We systematically study the strange and charm vector mesons $K^{*}$, $\phi$, $D^{*}$, $D_s^{*}$, and $J/\psi$ and the dependence of their spin alignment on transverse momentum, rapidity, temperature, baryon chemical potential, and angular velocity. We find that the heavy charm vector mesons $D^{*}$, $D_s^{*}$, and $J/\psi$ mesons exhibit $\rho_{00}>1/3$ at low transverse momentum, whereas the light strange vector mesons $K^{*}$ and $\phi$ exhibit the opposite low-momentum behavior and angular distributions with $\rho_{00}<1/3$. We trace this flavor-dependent separation to the different locations of the vacuum mass shell relative to the thermally shifted longitudinal and transverse spectral peaks. The results qualitatively reproduce several trends observed at low and intermediate transverse momentum. Spin alignment is insensitive to baryon chemical potential and only weakly affected by angular velocity. These results establish an equilibrium holographic baseline for vector-meson spin alignment across flavor sectors and help delineate the regimes in which additional mechanisms, such as nonequilibrium evolution, fluctuations, and hard production, become important.

一句话导读：通过四味软墙全息模型研究矢量介子自旋排列的介质效应，发现奇异与粲介子低横动量行为相反，并建立平衡全息基线。

相关度：low — 论文属于全息 QCD/核物理中的重离子介子自旋排列研究，与用户重点标签（如全息超流、相变、QNM等）无直接重叠。

DBI暴胀中局域特征产生的原初黑洞与诱导引力波

Primordial black holes and induced gravitational waves from localized features in DBI inflation

Narges Rashidi · astro-ph.CO, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们在 Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) 暴胀框架下研究原初黑洞的形成，其中背景动力学由暴胀子势 V(φ) 和翘曲因子 f(φ) 的显式函数形式决定。数值求解背景方程得到慢滚参数的演化。我们证明，势中的局域特征伴随翘曲因子中的相关结构，会动力学地诱发慢滚参数的瞬态压低，从而导致短命的非吸引子阶段。这种行为在小尺度上增强曲率功率谱，同时保持与 CMB 尺度可观测量的相容性。随后数值求解 Mukhanov-Sasaki 方程计算所得功率谱，结果显示在不同质量范围内 PBH 丰度出现窄而局域的峰。我们还评估了相关的诱导引力波随机背景，发现预测信号可以落入未来脉冲星计时阵列和空间干涉仪的灵敏度带内，具体取决于暴胀特征所处的尺度。在 (r, n_s) 平面上的参数扫描表明，大尺度预言与当前观测约束一致。

English: We investigate primordial black hole formation in a Dirac-Born-Infeld inflationary framework in which the background dynamics are determined by explicit functional forms of the inflaton potential $V(\phi)$ and the warp factor $f(\phi)$. The background equations are solved numerically to obtain the evolution of the slow-roll parameters. We show that a localized feature in the potential, accompanied by a correlated structure in the warp factor, dynamically induces a transient suppression of the slow-roll parameter, leading to a short-lived non-attractor phase. This behavior generates an enhancement of the curvature power spectrum on small scales, while preserving consistency with CMB-scale observables. The Mukhanov-Sasaki equation is then solved numerically to compute the resulting power spectrum, which exhibits narrow and localized peaks in the PBH abundance across different mass ranges. We also evaluate the associated stochastic background of induced gravitational waves and find that the predicted signal can fall within the sensitivity bands of future pulsar timing arrays and space-based interferometers, depending on the scale of the inflationary feature. A parameter scan in the $(r,n_s)$ plane shows that the large-scale predictions remain consistent with current observational constraints.

一句话导读：在 DBI 暴胀中引入局域的势与翘曲因子结构，可增强小尺度曲率功率谱并产生原初黑洞与可被未来探测器观测的诱导引力波。

相关度：low — 论文属于宇宙学暴胀与原初黑洞/引力波方向，与用户重点标签中的全息凝聚态、超流超导等支线不相关；仅因涉及引力波和黑洞现象而与经典引力/广义相对论支线有微弱关联。

NS-UNO: 基于不受观测数量限制的中子星状态方程推断

NS-UNO: Neutron Star EoS Inference from an Unconstrained Number of Observations

Val\'eria Carvalho, M\'arcio Ferreira, Micha{\l} Bejger, Constan\c{c}a Provid\^encia · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：未来中子星（NS）的多信使观测预计将大幅增加对致密物质状态方程（EoS）的天体物理约束的数量和精度。这促使推断框架能够容纳可变、非固定数量的观测，同时保留每次测量相关的后验信息。在这项工作中，我们介绍了NS-UNO，一种用于NS EoS推断的神经后验估计框架，旨在适应不受观测数量限制（UNO）的情况。NS-UNO将层次化DeepSets模型与条件归一化流相结合，使得一个训练好的模型能够对不同大小的质量-半径观测集进行推断，每个观测由一组后验样本表示。我们展示了使用联合训练的分段多方和参数化高斯过程EoS集成所获得的高精度且校准良好的后验重建。随着观测覆盖更广的中子星质量范围，重建效果得到改善，同时对观测数量和精度的变化保持鲁棒。该模型还能推广到训练中未使用的EoS族。最后，我们定性演示了该框架在来自NICER和GW170817的当前多信使约束上的应用。NS-UNO提供了一种灵活且可扩展的NS EoS推断方法，自然适用于下一代多信使天文学预期日益多样化的观测数据集。

English: Future multimessenger observations of neutron stars (NS) are expected to substantially increase both the number and precision of astrophysical constraints on the equation of state (EoS) of dense matter. This motivates inference frameworks capable of accommodating a variable, non fixed number of observations while preserving the posterior information associated with each measurement. In this work, we introduce NS-UNO, a Neural Posterior Estimation framework for NS EoS inference designed to accommodate an Unconstrained Number of Observations (UNO). NS-UNO combines a hierarchical DeepSets model with a conditional normalising flow, enabling a single trained model to perform inference from mass-radius observation sets of varying size, with each observation represented by a set of posterior samples. We demonstrate accurate and well calibrated posterior reconstructions using a model trained jointly on piecewise polytropic and non-parametric Gaussian process EoS ensembles. The reconstruction improves as observations probe a broader range of NS masses, while remaining robust to variations in the number and precision of the observations. The model also generalises to EoSs outside the families used during training. Finally, we qualitatively demonstrate the framework on current multimessenger constraints from NICER and GW170817. NS-UNO provides a flexible and scalable approach to NS EoS inference, naturally suited to the increasingly diverse observational datasets expected from next generation multimessenger astronomy.

一句话导读：提出NS-UNO，一种基于神经后验估计的中子星状态方程推断框架，可处理任意数量的观测样本，并在多信使数据上验证。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典引力背景下中子星状态方程的推断方法，使用机器学习技术，但与全息或量子引力无关。

检查致密恒星天体 HESS J1731-347 中厚壳层的可能性

Examining the possibility of a thick crust in the compact stellar object HESS J1731-347

Sebastian Kubis, W{\l}odzimierz W\'ojcik · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.SR · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：先前已证明，带有同位旋-标量和同位旋-标量介子交叉项的相对论平均场模型，即克拉科夫交叉项（CCT）模型，能够解释轻而致密的天体 HESS J1731-347。在此，该模型补充了对相变的正确处理。应用热力学一致的吉布斯条件表明，在中子星核心处，混合相系统在一个很宽的密度范围内出现，一直延伸到中子星壳层，从而产生一种具有厚重壳层的有趣恒星结构。

English: A relativistic mean-field model with a crossing term of isovector-scalar and isoscalar mesons, the Cracow crossing terms (CCT) model, has previously been shown to be capable of explaining the light and compact object HESS J1731-347 [11]. Here, the model is supplemented with a correct treatment of the phase transition. Applying the thermodynamically consistent Gibbs conditions shows that a mixed-phase system occurs in the core of the neutron star over a wide range of densities, extending up to the neutron star crust, leading to an intriguing stellar structure with a thick and massive crust.

一句话导读：利用热力学一致的吉布斯条件，CCT模型中子星核心在宽密度范围出现混合相，导致厚重且大质量的壳层。

相关度：low — 论文研究非全息框架下的中子星状态方程与相变，仅涉及经典引力背景，与全息/量子引力主线无直接关联。

月球与卫星激光测距对旋进双星合并参数的重构

Inspiralling Binary merger parameter reconstruction with lunar and satellite Laser Ranging

Miguel Vanvlasselaer · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：对地月距离和地球-卫星距离的精密跟踪为探测旋进大质量黑洞双星产生的引力波提供了一种新颖的低频途径，是对空间干涉仪的补充。本文首次研究了从合成距离观测中重构双星参数的问题。设置：假设地球-卫星双星为理想的无扰动开普勒运动，我们建立了一个框架，从这些测距观测量所受的扰动中重构啁啾质量 M_c 和光度距离 D_L，并以月球激光测距（LLR）和双卫星测距配置（GUEST）作为具体案例研究。结果：我们发现，对于 LLR 和 GUEST，啁啾质量的重构都按 SNR 的幂律紧密收敛（σ_{M_c}/M_c ∝ SNR^α，α ≈ -1 到 -1.8）。相比之下，在 LLR 配置中，光度距离与天空方向角存在严重的、与 SNR 无关的简并，导致分数偏差和不确定度达百分之几十到超过百分之百，与信号强度无关。双卫星 GUEST 配置打破了这一简并，并恢复了 D_L 的收敛的、依赖于 SNR 的重构，其缩放行为与啁啾质量相当。这些结果表明，多基线测距架构对于从基于测距的引力波搜索中提取光度距离信息至关重要，而即使单条测距基线（如地月系统）也能可靠地测量啁啾质量。

English: Precision tracking of Earth--Moon and Earth--satellite ranges offers a novel, low-frequency avenue for detecting gravitational waves from inspiralling massive black hole binaries, complementary to space-based interferometers. In this paper, we propose a first study of the reconstruction of the binary parameters from a synthetic set of range observations. \textit{Set-up}: Assuming an ideal unperturbed Keplerian motion for the earth-satellite binary, we develop a framework for reconstructing the chirp mass $\mathcal M_c$ and luminosity distance $D_L$ of chirping sources from the perturbations they induce on ranging observables, using both Lunar Laser Ranging (LLR) and a two-satellite ranging configuration (GUEST) as concrete case studies. \textit{Result}: We find that chirp-mass recovery tightly converges as a power law in SNR ($\sigma_{M_c}/M_c \propto \mathrm{SNR}^{\alpha}$, $\alpha \simeq -1$ to $-1.8$) for both LLR and GUEST. In contrast, the luminosity distance suffers from a severe, SNR-independent degeneracy with the sky-orientation angles in the LLR configuration, yielding fractional biases and uncertainties of tens to over a hundred percent regardless of signal strength. The two-satellite GUEST configuration breaks this degeneracy and restores a convergent, SNR-dependent recovery of $D_L$ comparable in scaling to that of the chirp mass. These results demonstrate that multi-baseline ranging architectures are essential for extracting luminosity-distance information from ranging-based gravitational wave searches, while chirp-mass measurements remain reliably accessible even with a single ranging baseline such as the Earth--Moon system.

一句话导读：研究了利用月球和卫星激光测距重构双星啁啾质量与光度距离的可行性，发现双卫星配置可解决光度距离简并问题。

相关度：low — 论文涉及经典引力波探测与参数估计，与全息原理或理论全息研究无关，但与广义相对论经典引力支线相关。

与非奇异脉动坍缩相关的稳态旋转几何

Stationary Rotating Geometries Associated with Nonsingular Pulsating Collapse

A. D. Souza, F. A. Brito, J. Spinelly · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：自广义相对论（GR）得到首批精确解以来，该理论就预言了一类新的致密天体：黑洞。Hawking--Penrose奇点定理表明，在适当假设下，GR中的引力坍缩会导致奇异行为。受这类情形可能需要超越经典GR物理的预期启发，受环量子宇宙学（LQC）和膜世界情景启发的有效方法引入了高密度修正。本文考虑 Gao, Lu, Shen, 和 Faraoni 引入的静态外部几何，它对应于一个通过一系列坍缩和反弹阶段避免奇点的共动内部模型。利用 Azreg-Aïnou 形式中的 Newman--Janis 算法，我们构造了这一几何的稳态旋转外部延拓。所得线元描述一个稳态轴对称旋转时空，其视界几何、角速度、表面引力和 Hawking 温度在量子/膜世界修正可忽略（l→0）时恢复 Kerr 和 Schwarzschild 极限。我们进一步分析了固定旋转状态空间参数下的热响应，识别出 Davies 型奇异点，并建立了一个扩展的热力学状态空间关系。

English: Since the first exact solutions of General Relativity (GR) were obtained, it became clear that the theory predicts a new class of compact objects: black holes. The Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems show that, under appropriate assumptions, gravitational collapse in GR leads to singular behavior. Motivated by the expectation that such regimes require physics beyond classical GR, effective approaches inspired by Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) and braneworld scenarios introduce high-density corrections. In this work we consider the static exterior geometry introduced by Gao, Lu, Shen, and Faraoni, associated with a comoving interior model in which the singularity is avoided through a sequence of collapse and bounce phases. Using the Newman--Janis algorithm in the Azreg-A\"inou formulation, we construct a stationary rotating exterior extension of this geometry. The resulting line element describes a stationary axisymmetric rotating spacetime whose horizon geometry, angular velocity, surface gravity, and Hawking temperature recover the Kerr and Schwarzschild limits when the quantum/braneworld correction becomes negligible ($l\to 0$). We further analyze the thermal response at fixed rotational state-space parameter, identify Davies-type singular points, and formulate an extended thermodynamic state-space relation.

一句话导读：构造带量子/膜世界修正的非奇异脉动坍缩外部的稳态旋转几何，并在修正消失时回到Kerr/Schwarzschild极限，同时分析其热力学性质。

相关度：high — 论文属于用户重点分支“经典引力/广义相对论”，研究稳态旋转黑洞时空及其热力学，直接匹配该标签。

负虚轴上的 Schwarzschild 谱阶梯：端点非选择性、割线相位与 Jost 分类

Schwarzschild spectral ladders on the negative imaginary axis: Endpoint nonselection, branch-cut phase, and Jost classification

Davide Batic, Denys Dutykh, Mark Essa Sukaiti · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：紧致化谱离散化可能产生稳定的负虚轴（NIA）本征值阶梯，但有限矩阵收敛并不能确定准正则极点。对于轴向 Schwarzschild 扰动，出入波分解会留下一个在 Ω=-iα 处行为如 t^{4α} 的不需要的视界解，以及一个 C^∞ 平坦的不需要的无穷远解。因此端点 C^k 正则性在 4α>k 时不具选择性，故在 α≃15--32 区间上，C^2 连续统问题不可能有离散的 NIA 谱。精确 Chebyshev 网格公式给出的根-网格最大半径为 O(n^{2p})，其中 x∼C(1-y)^{-p}，这解释了 C1（x=2/(1-y)）与 C2（x=4/(1-y)^2）的差异。尽管如此，任意精度铅笔计算仍揭示了一个可复现的 68 点 C1 阶梯，而 C2 会对其重组。在每个 C1 频率处，两个物理横向 Jost 行列式都保持稳定非零。0.05 间隔的割线扫描未发现零点，精化幅角原理计算在两个连续条带中均给出零环绕数。这拒绝了全部 68 个候选，尽管网格与条带证据并非区间证明。相同阻尼对照恢复了 Schwarzschild QNM n=60,100,130，其 |\widehat{\mathcal D}|=10^{-48}--10^{-56}。该阶梯具有表面引力四分之一间距，遵循无参数 Casals--Ottewill 割线强度相位，并与 QNM n=62,...,129 的阻尼投影配对。在其第一个成员处，有限频率计算发现分支强度零点位于 α_q=15.07832396512359，介于渐近预测与 C1 根之间，支持但不证明序列范围的割线相位锁定。因此，精确的有限铅笔本征值可能无法通过不变的 Jost/Evans 极点判据。表示依赖的带 Keldysh 权重的节点是否会集体收敛到 Schwarzschild 割线响应和 Price 尾巴，仍有待探索。

English: Compactified spectral discretisations may yield stable negative-imaginary-axis (NIA) eigenvalue ladders, but finite-matrix convergence does not establish quasinormal poles. For axial Schwarzschild perturbations, ingoing--outgoing factorisation leaves an unwanted horizon solution behaving as $t^{4\alpha}$ at $\Omega=-i\alpha$, and a $C^\infty$-flat unwanted infinity solution. Thus endpoint $C^k$ regularity is nonselective for $4\alpha>k$, so the $C^2$ continuum problem cannot have a discrete NIA spectrum over $\alpha\simeq15$--32. Exact Chebyshev-grid formulas give roots-grid maximum radius $O(n^{2p})$ for $x\sim C(1-y)^{-p}$, explaining C1, $x=2/(1-y)$, versus C2, $x=4/(1-y)^2$. Arbitrary-precision pencils nevertheless reveal a reproducible 68-point C1 ladder, while C2 reorganises it. Both physical lateral Jost determinants remain stably nonzero at every C1 frequency. A 0.05-spaced on-cut scan finds no zero, and refined argument-principle calculations give zero winding in both continuation strips. This rejects all 68 candidates, although the mesh and strip evidence is not interval-certified. Same-damping controls recover Schwarzschild QNMs $n=60,100,130$, with $|\widehat{\mathcal D}|=10^{-48}$--$10^{-56}$. The ladder has surface-gravity quarter spacing, follows the parameter-free Casals--Ottewill branch-cut-strength phase, and pairs with damping projections of QNMs $n=62,\ldots,129$. At its first member, a finite-frequency calculation finds a branch-strength zero at $\alpha_q=15.07832396512359$, between the asymptotic prediction and the C1 root, supporting, but not proving, sequence-wide cut-phase locking. Thus accurate finite-pencil eigenvalues can fail the invariant Jost/Evans pole criterion. Whether representation-dependent nodes with Keldysh weights converge collectively to the Schwarzschild cut response and Price tail remains open.

一句话导读：通过 Jost/Evans 极点判据与任意精度计算，说明 Schwarzschild 轴向扰动在负虚轴上由紧致化谱离散化得到的 68 个候选本征值均非真实准正则极点，并指出这类表示依赖的阶梯与割线响应及 Price 尾巴的关系仍开放。

相关度：medium — 研究 Schwarzschild 黑洞轴向扰动的 QNM 与负虚轴类谱阶梯，涉及谱离散化收敛性与 Jost 判据，属于经典引力与 QNM 方向，但与用户核心全息模型无直接关联。

Schwarzschild时空中大自旋粒子的精确动力学与自旋墙

Exact dynamics and the spin wall for large-spin particles in Schwarzschild spacetime

Chao-Jun Feng, Rui-Hui Lin · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：弯曲时空中旋转测试粒子的运动由Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon (MPD)方程控制，并在自旋的一阶就偏离测地线。现有研究基本将动力学截断到自旋的线性阶，而我们在Tulczyjew--Dixon自旋补充条件下，对Schwarzschild时空中的平面运动进行了精确、非微扰处理：消去四速度将MPD系统重写为闭合代数形式，并将径向运动化为有效势问题，整个过程不进行任何自旋展开。这一精确框架揭示了线性化描述中缺失且无法触及的定性特征。最显著的是，对于足够大的自旋，有效势在由粒子质量和自旋决定的特征半径处出现双根，标志着一个纯自旋起源的不可穿透的“自旋墙”；超过临界自旋后，该墙位于事件视界之外，并屏蔽一般下落粒子。此外，自旋墙对粒子具有过滤作用：只有自旋、角动量和能量满足特定组合的轨道才能穿透，其他轨道在到达视界之前被反射。同时，线性处理中随自旋连续移动的最内稳定圆轨道，在弱场极限下得到闭式解，并证明在足够大的自旋下不再存在。我们还计算了弱场极限下近日点进动的自旋修正，并验证所有结果在零自旋时退化到标准形式。自旋墙及其过滤规则是真正的非微扰现象，对自旋的任何有限阶展开均不可见，可能在致密天体周围吸积流中产生可观测特征。

English: The motion of a spinning test particle in a curved spacetime is governed by the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon (MPD) equations and deviates from geodesic motion already at first order in the spin. While essentially all existing studies truncate the dynamics at linear order in the spin, we present an exact, nonperturbative treatment of planar motion in Schwarzschild spacetime under the Tulczyjew--Dixon spin supplementary condition: eliminating the four-velocity recasts the MPD system into a closed algebraic form and reduces the radial motion to an effective-potential problem, with no expansion in the spin at any stage. This exact framework uncovers qualitative features that are absent from---and in fact unattainable within---the linearized description. Most notably, for sufficiently large spin the effective potential develops a double root at a characteristic radius determined solely by the particle mass and spin, marking an impenetrable \emph{spin wall} of purely spin origin; beyond a critical spin the wall lies outside the event horizon and shields it from generic infalling particles. Moreover, the wall is a filter for particle: only orbits with a specific combinations of spin, angular momentum and energy can penetrate it, all others being reflected before reaching the horizon. In addition, the innermost stable circular orbit, which in linear treatments merely shifts continuously with spin, is obtained in closed form in the weak-field limit and is shown to cease to exist at sufficiently large spin. We further compute the spin correction to the perihelion precession in the weak-field limit and verify that all results reduce to the standard ones at vanishing spin. The spin wall and its filtering rule are genuine nonperturbative phenomena, invisible to any finite-order expansion in the spin, with potential observational signatures in accretion flows around compact objects.

一句话导读：对Schwarzschild时空中旋转测试粒子运动进行精确非微扰求解，发现纯自旋起源的不可穿透“自旋墙”及其过滤机制，并得到最内稳定圆轨道和近日点进动的闭式结果。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典引力中的自旋粒子运动，属于广义相对论范畴，但非全息或量子引力，与用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论相关。

由全息暗能量支持的可穿越虫洞的Yukawa修正

Yukawa modification of Traversable Wormholes supported by Holographic Dark Energy

Remo Garattini, Phongpichit Channuie, Kirill Zatrimaylov · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：受全息暗能量模型启发，我们扩展了参考文献[1]中的分析，考虑了不同能量密度(HDE)剖面上的Yukawa形变。我们探索的剖面包括：Bekenstein-Hawking HDE、Moradpour能量密度、标准Renyi HDE和混合能量密度。借助非均匀状态方程 p_r(r)=ω_r(r)ρ(r)，我们考虑了零潮汐力。与原始情况不同，仅Bekenstein-Hawking HDE剖面族中的一个特定情况在r→∞时出现发散的ω_r(r)。为了消除该发散，我们在远距离引入了修正，以获得有限结果。这意味着零潮汐力可以在非常大但有限的时空区域内实现。该修正未改变喉部附近状态方程的行为。对于每个方案，我们计算了应力-能量张量的分量。我们发现Yukawa-Bekenstein-Hawking HDE是唯一正定的剖面，而Yukawa-Moradpour和Yukawa-Renyi仅在有限区域内能量密度为正。剩余的Yukawa-Mixed能量密度始终为负。这意味着Yukawa形变引入了一种负能量密度。

English: Inspired by holographic dark energy models, we extend the analysis performed in Ref.\cite{RGPC} taking into account Yukawa deformations on the different energy density (HDE) profiles. The profiles we have explored are: the Bekenstein-Hawking HDE, the Moradpour energy density, the Standard Renyi HDE and the Mixed Energy Density. With the help of an inhomogeneous Equation of State of the form $p_{r}(r)=\omega _{r}\left( r\right) \rho (r)$, we have considered Zero Tidal Forces. Differently from the original case, only a particular case of the family of the Bekenstein-Hawking HDE profiles distorted by the Yukawa term has developed a divergent $\omega _{r}\left( r\right) $ for $r\rightarrow \infty $. To cure such a divergence we have introduced a modification at large distances in order to have a finite result. This means that the Zero Tidal Forces can be imposed in a very large but limited region of the spacetime. Such modification did not change the behavior of the Equation of State close to the throat. For every proposal we have computed the components of the Stress-Energy Tensor. What we have found is that the Yukawa-Bekenstein-Hawking HDE is the only positive profile, while the Yukawa-Moradpour and the Yukawa-Renyi have a limited region where the energy density is positive. The remaining Yukawa-Mixed Energy Density is always negative. This means that the Yukawa distortion introduces a kind of negative energy density.

一句话导读：研究全息暗能量剖面在Yukawa修正下对可穿越虫洞的支持，发现仅特定情况满足零潮汐力且能量密度正定。

相关度：low — 论文涉及全息暗能量模型在虫洞中的应用，但用户重点标签集中于全息超流超导、相变、QNM等方向，未见直接重叠；属于经典引力/广义相对论领域，但与用户关注点不符。

旋转 Konoplya-Zhidenko 黑洞时空中的共振束缚轨道与 kludge 波形

Resonant bound orbits and kludge waveforms in rotating Konoplya-Zhidenko black hole spacetime

Qiyu Dai, Xiongjun Fang, Xiao-Mei Kuang, Jiliang Jing · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们在旋转的 Konoplya-Zhidenko (KZ) 黑洞时空中研究了类时束缚运动、共振周期轨道及其引力波特征。利用 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程的可分离性，我们用 $(p,e,z_1)$ 参数化偏心且倾斜的束缚轨道，推导相应的运动常数 $(E,L_z,Q)$，并利用轨道频率识别共振位形。我们研究了一系列共振，包括倾斜轨道的径向-极向共振和赤道偏心轨道的径向-方位角共振。我们进一步为具有代表性的赤道共振轨道构造物理标定的四极-kludge 波形，并分析其频域特征。我们的结果表明，KZ 形变使共振位置发生移动，并同时修改了轨道轨迹和所产生的引力波信号。相应的特征应变主要位于毫赫兹频段，使这些信号处于天基引力波探测器相关的频率范围。

English: We investigate timelike bound motion, resonant periodic orbits, and their gravitational-wave signatures in the rotating Konoplya-Zhidenko (KZ) black hole spacetime. Using the separability of the Hamilton-Jacobi equation, we parameterize eccentric and inclined bound orbits by $(p,e,z_1)$, derive the corresponding constants of motion $(E,L_z,Q)$, and use the orbital frequencies to identify resonant configurations. We study a range of resonances, including radial-polar resonances of inclined orbits and radial-azimuthal resonances of equatorial eccentric orbits. We further construct physically scaled quadrupole-kludge waveforms for representative equatorial resonant orbits and analyze their frequency-domain characteristics. Our results show that the KZ deformation shifts the resonance locations and modifies both the orbital trajectories and the resulting gravitational-wave signals. The corresponding characteristic strain lies predominantly in the millihertz band, placing these signals in the frequency range relevant to space-based gravitational wave detectors.

一句话导读：在旋转 Konoplya-Zhidenko 黑洞时空中研究束缚轨道与共振的引力波特征，发现形变会改变共振位置，波形落在毫赫兹频段。

相关度：high — 论文直接属于用户指定的‘经典引力/广义相对论’研究支线，专注于旋转黑洞的束缚轨道和引力波波形，与经典引力观测相关。

旋转Weyl共形黑洞中的能量提取过程与热力学

Energy extraction processes and thermodynamics in rotating Weyl conformal black holes

Dilmurod Ortiqboev, Farruh Atamurotov, Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov, Chen Zhou, G. Mustafa · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：本工作研究了共形Weyl引力框架下的原始Penrose过程(PP)以及黑洞(BH)热力学。该理论的特征在于有量纲常数$\gamma$（在原本渐近平直的时空中编码星系旋转曲线的渐近旋转曲率）和$\kappa$（与宇宙学背景常数$\Lambda$相关）。我们确定了该理论中旋转黑洞的事件视界和极端构型的位置，并给出了事件视界半径的微扰和精确表达式。测地方程被限制在赤道平面上，相应的动力学（包括能量提取过程）仅在该平面上研究。作为主要结果，我们检验了原始PP的可行性和效率，分析了黑洞减速机制，并在共形Weyl引力的背景下检验了黑洞热力学四定律的有效性。

English: This work investigates the original Penrose process (PP), as well as black hole (BH) thermodynamics, within the framework of conformal Weyl gravity. The theory is characterized by the dimensionful constants $\gamma$, which encodes the asymptotic rotational curvature of galactic rotation curves in an otherwise asymptotically flat spacetime, and $\kappa$, which is associated with the cosmological background constant $\Lambda$. We determine the locations of the event horizon and the extreme configurations for rotating BHs in this theory, and we provide both perturbative and exact expressions for the event horizon radius. The geodesic equations are restricted to the equatorial plane, and the corresponding dynamics, including energy extraction processes, are studied exclusively in this plane. As the principal results, we examine the feasibility and efficiency of the original PP, analyze BH deceleration mechanisms, and test the validity of the four laws of BH thermodynamics in the context of conformal Weyl gravity.

一句话导读：研究共形Weyl引力旋转黑洞的Penrose过程与热力学四定律。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的旋转黑洞能量提取与热力学研究，与用户重点的经典引力支线契合。

使用边界细化自动识别和扣除引力波毛刺

Automated identification and subtraction of gravitational-wave glitches using boundary refinement

Mohammad Abu Thaher Chowdhury, Soumya D Mohanty · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：引力波应变数据中的瞬态噪声伪影（即毛刺）会提高天体物理搜索的误报率，并在与信号重叠时降低参数估计质量。目前没有方法能够识别毛刺的时间边界：现有的检测和分类工具仅标记毛刺而不解析其范围，迫使扣除程序在填充窗口上运行，这既耗费时间又抹除了毛刺本身之外的信号。我们提出了三种边界识别方法——AMPS（基于幅度的多毛刺脉冲分割器）、FLARE（基于适应度的定位与细化提取器）和CRISP（基于频谱图功率的连通区域识别），并配以三种扣除技术：自适应样条拟合、小波收缩及其组合。这些方法应用于来自GravitySpy数据库的五个毛刺，覆盖Advanced LIGO前三次观测运行。AMPS通过鲁棒幅度阈值确定边界，FLARE通过分段样条拟合的最佳适应度确定边界，CRISP通过频谱图功率确定边界。在四个宽带毛刺上注入啁啾信号，组合技术恢复了注入信噪比的95%至97%，而单独使用小波收缩仅恢复约64%。CRISP在不同毛刺间给出了最均匀的边界宽度；AMPS和CRISP都能在不到一秒内确定边界，比FLARE快两到三个数量级。对于与GW170817重叠的毛刺，在默认边界下残留低频余量，这是避免移除信号功率的刻意权衡。散落光毛刺尚未测试，是未来工作的主要方向。

English: Transient noise artifacts, or glitches, in gravitational wave strain data elevate the false alarm rate of astrophysical searches and degrade parameter estimation when overlapping a signal. No method previously identified a glitch's time boundary: existing detection and classification tools flag and label glitches without resolving their extent, forcing subtraction to run over padded windows that cost time and erase signal beyond the glitch itself. We present three boundary identification methods, AMPS (Amplitude-based Multi-glitch Pulse Segmenter), FLARE (Fitness-based Localization And Refinement Extractor), and CRISP (connected-region identification via spectrogram power), paired with three subtraction techniques, adaptive spline fitting, wavelet shrinkage, and their combination, across five glitches from the GravitySpy database spanning Advanced LIGO's first three observing runs. AMPS sets a boundary from a robust amplitude threshold, FLARE from the best fitness of a segmented spline fit, and CRISP from spectrogram power. Injecting a chirp signal on four broadband glitches, the combined technique recovers 95 to 97 percent of the injected signal-to-noise ratio, against roughly 64 percent for wavelet shrinkage alone. CRISP gives the most uniform boundary width across glitches; AMPS and CRISP both identify a boundary in a fraction of a second, two to three orders of magnitude faster than FLARE. For the glitch overlapping GW170817, a low-frequency residual persists under the default boundary, a deliberate tradeoff against removing signal power. Scattered light glitches remain untested and are the primary direction for future work.

一句话导读：提出三种毛刺边界识别方法和三种扣除技术，以提高引力波观测中重叠信号的信噪比恢复。

相关度：low — 论文专注于引力波数据中的噪声伪影识别与扣除，属于经典引力实验数据处理，不涉及全息或量子引力理论。

黑洞铃宕中有多少来自光环内部？

How much of the black hole ringdown is sourced within the light ring?

Mark Ho-Yeuk Cheung · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：并不多。有人提出，对黑洞铃宕引力波形施加有理滤波可以揭示源自视界附近的直接波成分。通过求解点粒子坠入Kerr黑洞时的Teukolsky方程，我表明无论是否滤波，源自顺行光环内部的波的最大振幅不超过全轨迹源的波的约10%。这与准正规模被解释为绕光环传播的波、直接波是这些模的功率在时间上的重新分配一致。因此，准正规模和直接波都更多地是光环的探针，而非近视界区域的探针。对于GW250114，我估计峰值后滤波波形中源自光环内部的波成分的信噪比ρ≲1。

English: Not much. It has been proposed that applying a rational filter to a black hole ringdown gravitational waveform reveals a direct wave component sourced near the event horizon. By solving the Teukolsky equation for a point particle plunging into a Kerr black hole, I show that the maximum amplitude of the waves sourced within the prograde light ring is $\lesssim 10\%$ that of those sourced by the full trajectory no matter if the waves are filtered or not. This agrees with the interpretation of quasinormal modes as waves orbiting the light ring and the direct wave as the power of these modes redistributed in time. Therefore, both quasinormal modes and direct waves are more a probe of the light ring than the near-horizon region. For GW250114, I estimate that the component of the waves sourced within the light ring have a signal-to-noise ratio $\rho \lesssim 1$ measured after the peak of the filtered waves.

一句话导读：通过Teukolsky方程计算指出，克尔黑洞铃宕中来自顺行光环内部的波形成分最多约10%，说明QNM与直接波主要探测光环而非视界附近。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典黑洞摄动与铃宕直接波来源，重点关注QNM和光环物理，与用户重点标签中的QNM与经典引力支线直接相关；不涉及全息或量子引力。

非零宇宙学常数下爱因斯坦标量场方程的剥离性质

Peeling property for the Einstein scalar field equations with the nonzero cosmological constant

Jialue Li, Xiao Zhang · gr-qc, math.DG · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：受引力波、暗物质和暗能量相互作用的启发，我们研究了Bondi-Sachs度规下具有非零宇宙学常数的爱因斯坦标量场方程。我们在出射辐射条件下提供了渐近展开。与零宇宙学常数的情况不同，渐近展开依赖于四个额外的Λ无关函数B(u, θ, φ), X̃(u, θ, φ), Ỹ(u, θ, φ)和Ĩ(u)。我们表明，在适当的坐标变换下，可以令B(u, θ, φ)=0。当Ĩ=0时，我们证明了爱因斯坦标量场方程的剥离性质，并推导了非零宇宙学常数下Bondi能量-动量的损失公式。我们注意到，对于来自引力波的某些真实数据，该损失公式可能表明Bondi能量-动量的损失性质。

English: Inspired by interaction of gravitational waves, dark matters and dark energy, we study the Einstein scalar field equations with the nonzero cosmological constant for the Bondi-Sachs metrics. We provide the asymptotic expansions under the outgoing radiation condition. Unlike the case of the zero cosmological constant, the asymptotic expansions depend on four additional $\Lambda$-independent functions $B(u, \theta, \phi)$, $\tilde{X}(u, \theta, \phi)$, $\tilde{Y}(u, \theta, \phi)$ and $\tilde{I}(u)$. We show that, under suitable coordinate transformation, we can make $B(u, \theta, \phi)=0$. We prove the peeling property for the Einstein scalar field equations if $\tilde{I}=0$, and derive a loss formula of the Bondi energy-momentum for the nonzero cosmological constant. We remark that, for certain real data from gravitational waves, the loss formula is likely to indicate the loss property of the Bondi energy-momentum.

一句话导读：研究非零宇宙学常数下爱因斯坦标量场方程在Bondi-Sachs框架中的渐近展开与剥离性质，并给出Bondi能量-动量损失公式。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典广义相对论中的爱因斯坦标量场方程、Bondi-Sachs度规和剥离性质，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线。

同一种共振的两种语言：从核衰变到黑洞振铃

Two Languages for the Same Resonance: From Nuclear Decay to Black-Hole Ringdown

Okuto Morikawa · gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP, nucl-th, physics.hist-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：量子共振与黑洞准正规模（QNM）由密切相关的数学描述：辐射边界条件、格林函数或预解式的解析延拓、非物理黎曼面上的极点、以及非自伴谱表示。本文并不首次提出这种等价性，而是重构其谱系。核共振理论源于放射性衰变、反应截面、亚稳态复合态和复能量；而黑洞微扰理论源于时空稳定性与因果响应。我们将这两条发展置于共同的时间线上，从Sommerfeld辐射条件和早期核衰变理论，到Regge-Wheeler微扰、Vishveshwara 1968年的学位论文和1970年论文、Press 1971年的“quasi-normal”术语，以及独立的Aguilar-Balslev-Combes解析延拓理论，随后追溯它们在散射共振理论中的数学统一。除时间线外，我们还给出一个实用词典，对应无入射条件与视界入射/无穷远出射条件、极点能量与复频率、宽度与阻尼率、留数与激发因子、以及非共振背景与分支切割和即时贡献。目标是让跨学科等价可操作，同时不抹平其历史，并区分数学等价与历史谱系。

English: Quantum resonances and black-hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) are described by closely related mathematics: radiative boundary conditions, analytic continuation of Green functions or resolvents, poles on nonphysical sheets, and non-self-adjoint spectral representations. This article does not introduce that equivalence but reconstructs its genealogy. Nuclear resonance theory grew from radioactive decay, reaction cross sections, metastable compound states, and complex energies, whereas black-hole perturbation theory grew from spacetime stability and causal response. We place the two developments on a common chronology, from Sommerfeld's radiation condition and early nuclear-decay theory through Regge--Wheeler perturbations, Vishveshwara's 1968 dissertation and 1970 papers, Press's 1971 quasi-normal terminology, and the independent Aguilar--Balslev--Combes theory of analytic dilation, then follow their later mathematical reunification in scattering-resonance theory. Alongside the chronology, we give a working dictionary relating no-incoming and horizon-ingoing/infinity-outgoing conditions, pole energy and complex frequency, width and damping rate, residues and excitation factors, and nonresonant background with branch-cut and prompt contributions. The aim is to make the cross-disciplinary equivalence operational without flattening its history, and to distinguish mathematical equivalence from historical genealogy.

一句话导读：追溯核共振与黑洞准正规模之间数学等价性的历史与术语对照综述。

相关度：medium — 文章综述黑洞QNM与核共振的数学等价和历史，对经典引力/QNM背景有参考价值，但不涉及全息或新物理结果。

准拓扑引力的黑洞对应：阴影、准正则模、灰体因子

Black hole correspondences of quasi-topological gravity : Shadows, quasinormal modes, graybody factors

Sihao Fan, Chen Wu, Wenjun Guo · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在准拓扑引力框架下，我们系统研究了D=5维规则黑洞的三类可观测量的对应关系——阴影、准正则模和灰体因子。利用WKB近似和GrayHawk数值积分，我们检验了GBF-QNM对应和阴影-GBF对应在低模（l=2）和高维（D=5）下的适用性：GBF-QNM对应与数值结果之间的差异仍然很小；我们应用已知的Langer修正（将l替换为有效角动量κ=√(l(l+D-3))≈l+(D-3)/2），这使得阴影-GBF对应在低模下的误差降低到与GBF-QNM对应相当的水平。我们的工作将阴影-GBF对应扩展到高维规则黑洞，为从单一观测量预测量谱和霍金辐射轮廓提供了一条可行途径，从而实现对引力的多信使检验。

English: Within the framework of quasi-topological gravity, we systematically investigate the correspondences among three classes of observables---the shadow, the quasinormal modes, and the graybody factors---of regular black holes in $D = 5$ dimensions. Using the WKB approximation and GrayHawk numerical integration, we test the applicability of the GBF--QNM correspondence and the shadow--GBF correspondence in the low-mode ($l=2$) and higher-dimensions ($D=5$): the difference between the GBF--QNM correspondence and the numerical results remains small in magnitude; we apply the known Langer correction (replacing $l$ by the effective angular momentum $\kappa = \sqrt{l(l+D-3)} \simeq l + (D-3)/2$), which reduces the error of the shadow--GBF correspondence at low modes to a level comparable with that of the GBF--QNM correspondence. Our work extends the shadow--GBF correspondence to higher-dimensional regular black holes, providing a viable route to predict the black hole spectrum and the Hawking radiation profile from a single observable, and thereby to realize multi-messenger tests of gravity.

一句话导读：利用WKB近似和数值积分，系统检验了准拓扑引力中5维规则黑洞的阴影、QNM与灰体因子之间的对应关系，并通过Langer修正提高低模下的精度。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典引力中的黑洞阴影、QNM和灰体因子对应关系，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，与用户重点标签直接相关。

分层致密天体的引力波回波：双壳层模型

Gravitational-Wave Echoes from Layered Compact Objects: A Double-Shell Model

Qi Su, Ding-Fang Zeng · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：分层是天体物理物体的普遍特征。鉴于当以可与外部固定位置探针时钟同步的时间概念观察时，物理黑洞保留了其前身恒星的分层结构，我们研究了由两个同心薄壳组成的致密天体的线性微扰和引力波（GW）回波。与单壳情况相比，双壳结构在有效势中引入了一个额外的势垒，并将波传播空间分成四个耦合的有效空腔。随着内壳质量比的增加，新的谱峰从回波频谱的高频侧进入；第二个及后续峰向更高频率移动；最低频率峰先向低频移动，然后返回其 q=0 位置。我们将这种变化模式称为谱峰排队。它的存在表明引力波回波可以作为探测所考虑致密天体内部结构的探针。

English: Layering is a ubiquitous feature of astrophysical objects. Motivated by the fact that physical black holes retain the layered structure of their progenitor stars when viewed in the time concepts synchronizable with the clock of an outside fixed-position probe, we investigate linear perturbations and gravitational-wave (GW) echoes from a compact object composed of two concentric thin shells. Compared with the single-shell case, the double-shell structure introduces an extra barrier in the effective potential and partitions the wave propagation space into four coupled effective cavities. As the mass ratio of the inner shell increases, new spectral peaks enter from the high-frequency side of the echo spectrum; the second and later peaks shift toward higher frequencies; and the lowest-frequency peak first shifts toward lower frequencies and then returns to its $q=0$ position. We call this variation pattern spectral-peak queueing (SQ). Its existence suggests that GW echoes can be used as probes for the internal structure of compact objects under consideration.

一句话导读：该论文研究了由两个同心薄壳组成的致密天体的引力波回波，发现双壳结构导致谱峰排队现象，可用于探测致密天体内部结构。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力中的引力波回波，属于广义相对论应用，与重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关。

利用中子星-黑洞并合约束中子星物态方程

Constraining equation of state of neutron star using neutron star-black hole mergers

Vasudev Dubey, Rahul Kashyap, Yugesh Bhoge · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们研究了中子星-黑洞（NSBH）并合引力波暂现事件中可观测的潮汐效应。众所周知，中子星的潮汐形变携带着物态方程以及中子星物质成分的信息。与双中子星并合相比（其中通过测量称为lambda tilde的参数来观测两颗中子星的联合效应），在中子星-黑洞并合中，我们可以清晰地测量中子星分量的潮汐形变率。我们研究了在近期和远期规划的引力波探测器下测量中子星裸潮汐形变率的可行性。我们使用参数估计计算框架Bilby，对当前和未来引力波探测器网络中的数百个NSBH并合事件进行贝叶斯推断，并获得双星参数的后验概率分布函数。我们发现，在未来的探测器（包括爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者）中，至少需要20个事件才能清晰地区分中子星的物态方程。

English: In this work, we study tidal effects that can be observed in gravitational wave transient from neutron star-black hole (NSBH) mergers. It has been well known that neutron star's tidal deformation has imprint of equation of state and hence composition of neutron star matter. Compared to binary neutron star mergers, where we observe a combined effect of two neutron stars by measuring the parameter known as lambda tilde. In neutron star black hole mergers, we measure the tidal deformability of the neutron star component clearly. In this work, we study the feasibility of measuring the bare tidal deformability of a neutron star with planned gravitational detectors in the near and far future. We use the parameter estimation computation framework, Bilby is used to perform Bayesian inference for hundreds of NSBH mergers in current and future GW detector networks and obtain the posterior probability distribution functions of binary parameters. We find that we need at least 20 events to clearly distinguish the equation state of neutron stars in future detectors, including the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer.

一句话导读：通过模拟未来引力波探测器对中子星-黑洞并合的观测，评估测量中子星潮汐形变的能力，发现至少需要20个事件才能区分物态方程。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与广义相对论中的引力波和中子星物态方程研究，与用户关注的全息和量子引力主题无直接关联，但与经典引力支线相关。

Bekenstein-Hod界：来自GW250114的3.3σ确认

Bekenstein--Hod Bound: A $3.3\sigma$ Confirmation from GW250114

Hai-Tian Wang, Shao-Peng Tang, Yi-Zhong Fan · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：黑洞通过扰动系统弛豫速率的极限将引力、热力学和信息联系起来。Bekenstein-Hod界在固定温度下施加最小弛豫时间，但其观测检验需要黑洞热力学表征和衰减时间测量。这里我们利用GW250114（迄今观测到的最响亮的双黑洞并合引力波信号）检验该界。我们从峰前至少10M处截断的并合前数据推断残余温度，并从并合后数据推断其最长寿命衰减时间，从而避免直接重复使用相同的应变样本。我们允许振铃频率和阻尼时间独立变化，而不是固定为Kerr谱。对于主要的t_< = -10M分析，该界在焦点振铃起始时间上以3.3-3.6σ得到验证，比GW150914设定的91%置信水平有显著提高。结论在变化的并合前截断以及波形建模中显式包含短寿命第一泛音时仍稳健。这种分离数据的测量将信息论弛豫界转化为对单个天体物理黑洞的精确检验。

English: Black holes link gravity, thermodynamics and information via limits on the relaxation rate of a perturbed system. The Bekenstein-Hod bound imposes a minimum relaxation time at fixed temperature, but its observational test demands both black hole thermodynamic characterization and decay time measurement. Here we test this bound with GW250114, the loudest gravitational-wave signal yet observed from a binary black-hole merger. We infer the remnant temperature from pre-merger data truncated at least $10\,M$ before the peak and its longest-lived decay time from post-merger data, thereby avoiding direct reuse of the same strain samples. The ringdown frequencies and damping times are allowed to vary independently rather than being fixed to the Kerr spectrum. For the primary $t_{<}=-10\,M$ analysis, the bound is verified at $3.3-3.6\sigma$ across the focal ringdown start times, representing a substantial improvement over the $91\%$ confidence level set by GW150914. The conclusion remains robust under varied pre-merger cutoffs and explicit inclusion of the short-lived first overtone in waveform modelling. This separated-data measurement converts an information-theoretic relaxation bound into a precision test of a single astrophysical black hole.

一句话导读：利用GW250114引力波事件检验Bekenstein-Hod界，以3.3-3.6σ置信度验证，显著优于GW150914。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典引力中的引力波与黑洞并合，并对Bekenstein-Hod界进行观测检验，直接属于经典引力/广义相对论及QNM相关支线，与用户重点标签高度匹配。

宇宙弦环在引力反作用下的演化

Evolution of Cosmic String Loops under Gravitational Backreaction

Lasse Gerblich, Richard A. Battye, E. P. S. Shellard · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：Nambu-Goto宇宙弦环通常会形成尖点（cusps），即弦瞬时达到光速的点。这些尖点产生强引力波爆发，其特征应变谱为$\tilde{h}(\omega)\propto (G\mu)\,\omega^{-4/3}$，使其成为引力波搜索的主要目标，其中$\mu$是弦的线密度。然而，当考虑引力反作用时，这一图像被修改。利用方便规范，我们将Nambu-Goto运动方程重新表述为环在其自身动态源引力场中运动的形式，从而实现了在反作用下环的首次连续数值演化。一个关键发现是，局部尖点在反作用下幸存。然而，在此形式中计算出的引力波形被显著修改，削弱了尖点爆发，并在$f_c\propto(G\mu)^{-3/2}$处引入高频截止。$f_c$以上的抑制降低了当前和近未来探测器中预期的信噪比，相对于未扰动的波形预测。

English: Nambu-Goto cosmic string loops generically develop cusps, points where the string momentarily reaches the speed of light. These cusps produce strong gravitational wave bursts with a characteristic strain spectrum $\tilde{h}(\omega)\propto (G\mu)\,\omega^{-4/3}$, making them prime targets for gravitational wave searches, where $\mu$ is the string mass per unit length. However, this picture is modified when one accounts for gravitational backreaction. Using a convenient gauge, we reformulate the Nambu-Goto equations of motion for a loop moving in its own dynamically sourced gravitational field, enabling the first continuous numerical evolution of loops under this backreaction. A key finding is that locally cusps survive backreaction. Nevertheless, the gravitational waveform calculated in this formalism is significantly modified, weakening the cusp burst and introducing a high-frequency cutoff at $f_c\propto(G\mu)^{-3/2}$. This suppression above $f_c$ reduces the expected signal-to-noise ratio in current and near-future detectors relative to unperturbed waveform predictions.

一句话导读：首次数值模拟显示引力反作用削弱宇宙弦环的尖点引力波爆发并引入高频截止。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力中的宇宙弦环动力学和引力波信号，直接对应经典引力/广义相对论主题。

对称约化广义相对论流体力学在静态时空中的熵稳定且保持物理约束的DGSEM方法

Entropy-Stable and Physical-Constraint-Preserving DGSEM for Symmetry-Reduced General-Relativistic Hydrodynamics on Stationary Spacetimes

Guosheng Fu, Jian-Guo Liu · math.NA, cs.NA, gr-qc, physics.comp-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们为预先给定的静态时空上的对称约化广义相对论流体力学开发了一种熵稳定且保持物理约束的间断伽辽金谱元方法。通过局部标准正交变换，流体变量被表示为相对论流体动力学代数及可容许集与空间度量无关的形式，而时空几何通过静态系数进入。这种分离使得熵守恒的狭义相对论通量可以与几何源项的相容离散相结合。在仿射张量积网格上，所得DGSEM是守恒的，并满足半离散熵不等式，而变换后的变量为物理约束保持提供了凸框架。对于实际稳定化，我们使用仅依赖于几何的因果速度，该速度足以用于经典局部Lax-Friedrichs熵耗散和保持物理约束的Lax-Friedrichs分裂。全离散方法将该稳定化与SSP龙格-库塔时间推进、振荡消除和保守的局部标准正交态缩放相结合。数值实验涵盖了光滑和强激波的狭义相对论流、轴对称射流、稳态Michel吸积、Schwarzschild Bondi-Hoyle流以及四个Kerr吸积算例。结果展示了在光滑区域设计的高阶精度以及在弯曲静态背景上对苛刻相对论流的稳健性能。

English: We develop an entropy-stable and physical-constraint-preserving discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method for symmetry-reduced general-relativistic hydrodynamics on prescribed stationary spacetimes. Using a local orthonormal transformation, the fluid variables are expressed in a form for which the relativistic hydrodynamic algebra and the admissible set are independent of the spatial metric, while the spacetime geometry enters through stationary coefficients. This separation allows entropy-conservative special-relativistic fluxes to be combined with a compatible discretization of the geometric source terms. On affine tensor-product meshes, the resulting DGSEM is conservative and satisfies a semidiscrete entropy inequality, while the transformed variables provide a convex framework for physical-constraint preservation. For practical stabilization, we use a geometry-only causal speed that is sufficient for both classical local Lax--Friedrichs entropy dissipation and the physical-constraint-preserving Lax--Friedrichs splitting. The fully discrete method combines this stabilization with SSP Runge--Kutta time stepping, oscillation elimination, and conservative local-orthonormal-state scaling. Numerical experiments cover smooth and strongly shocked special-relativistic flows, an axisymmetric jet, stationary Michel accretion, Schwarzschild Bondi--Hoyle flow, and four Kerr accretion cases. The results demonstrate the designed high-order accuracy in smooth regimes and robust performance for demanding relativistic flows on curved stationary backgrounds.

一句话导读：发展了对称约化广义相对论流体力学在静态时空上的熵稳定且保持物理约束的DGSEM方法，并验证了其在弯曲时空中的高阶精度和稳健性。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的数值方法研究，与用户关注的全息/量子引力方向无关，但涉及黑洞吸积等经典引力背景，故相关度为中等。

f(R)引力中原初黑洞种子用于小红点

Primordial Black Hole Seeds for Little Red Dots in $f(R)$ Gravity

Saeed Fakhry · astro-ph.GA, astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）发现的大量致密小红点（LRD）和高红移超大质量黑洞，对标准爱丁顿限制增长从恒星遗迹形成提出了严峻的时间挑战。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个统一的混合组装框架，其中中等质量种子黑洞通过Hu-Sawicki f(R)引力中稠密星团内的层级原初黑洞（PBH）并合形成，随后进行星系气体吸积。我们证明，被屏蔽的f(R)第五力通过引力波辐射俘获核的二次增强非线性地加速早期种子形成，而环境变色龙屏蔽则动态自我调节增长。由于后续气体吸积与种子质量成乘法关系，f(R)种子增强在整个吸积历史中得以保留，从而大幅降低了在宇宙黎明前组装10^6-10^9太阳质量LRD所需的平均爱丁顿比率。将我们的活动质量函数与JWST数据对比显示，在现实的活动星系核占空比（δ≈10^-2）下，小的成团PBH比例（f_PBH≲10^-3）自然地匹配了z~5.5-6.5处观测到的LRD空间密度，同时完全符合LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的限值，且修改引力自然地驱动高质量尾。最后，我们表明，通过结合三种多信使诊断：遗迹自旋状态特征、随机引力波背景截止和PBH空间相关函数中的尺度依赖反转，可以清晰打破修改引力与星团密度之间的参数简并。

English: The discovery by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) of an abundant population of compact little red dots (LRDs) and supermassive black holes at high redshifts poses a severe timing challenge for standard Eddington-limited growth from stellar remnants. In this work, we present a unified hybrid assembly framework in which intermediate-mass seed black holes are formed via hierarchical primordial black hole (PBH) mergers within dense clusters in Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ gravity, followed by galactic gas accretion. We demonstrate that the screened $f(R)$ fifth force nonlinearly accelerates early seed formation through a quadratic enhancement of the gravitational-wave radiation-capture kernel, while environmental chameleon screening dynamically self-regulates the growth. Because subsequent gas accretion scales multiplicatively with seed mass, the $f(R)$ seed enhancement is preserved throughout the accretion history, drastically reducing the time-averaged Eddington ratios required to assemble $10^{6}\text{-}10^{9}\,M_{\odot}$ LRDs by cosmic dawn. Confronting our active mass functions with JWST data reveals that a small clustered PBH fraction ($f_{\rm PBH} \lesssim 10^{-3}$) under a realistic active galactic nucleus duty cycle ($\delta \approx 10^{-2}$) naturally matches observed LRD space densities at $z \sim 5.5\text{-}6.5$ while remaining fully compliant with LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA limits, with modified gravity naturally driving the high-mass tail. Finally, we show that parameter degeneracies between modified gravity and cluster density can be cleanly broken by combining three multi-messenger diagnostics: remnant spin state signatures, a stochastic GW background cutoff, and a scale-dependent inversion in the PBH spatial correlation function.

一句话导读：提出在f(R)引力中通过原初黑洞并合形成种子黑洞，以解释JWST观测到的高红移小红点和超大质量黑洞。

相关度：medium — 论文研究f(R)修改引力下的原初黑洞种子形成和引力波现象，涉及经典引力理论但非标准广义相对论，与用户重点标签'经典引力/广义相对论'部分相关。

早期宇宙中引力波与手性阿尔文波的共振动力学

Resonant Dynamics of Gravitational Wave and Chiral Alfv\'en wave in the Early Universe

Arun Kumar Pandey, Subalakshmi A, Sampurn Anand · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们研究了早期宇宙磁化手性等离子体中随机引力波(GW)与手性阿尔文波之间的共振相互作用。从耦合线性化引力的协变手性磁流体动力学出发，我们导出了手性-阿尔文速度与磁场扰动耦合的闭合方程组。解析和数值研究均表明，在两个由手性涡效应(CVE)劈裂的分支之和频处存在参数共振，且不稳定带宽度随引力波应变增大而变宽。该CVE-阿尔文比值并不是自由参数；在早期宇宙中，它由标准模型的相对论自由度确定。将等离子体对引力波的反反应纳入后，能量交换变为非线性，打破了通常的单频Manley-Rowe图像。对于自洽参数选择（反反应仅作为小修正），未被抑制的共振仍会在几个哈勃时间内驱动系统发生有限时间爆炸。我们随后计算了所涉及的共振频率和增长率，并描绘了当前及未来引力波探测器可能观测到的信号。

English: In this work, we investigate the resonant interaction between stochastic gravitational waves (GWs) and chiral Alfv\'en waves in a magnetized chiral plasma in the early Universe. Starting from covariant chiral magnetohydrodynamics coupled to linearized gravity, we derive a closed system of equations for the coupled chiral-Alfv\'en velocity and magnetic-field perturbations. Both analytics and numerics show a parametric resonance at the sum frequency of the two CVE-split branches, with an instability band that widens as the GW strain grows. This CVE-to-Alfv\'en ratio is not a free parameter. In the early Universe, it is fixed by the Standard Model's relativistic degrees of freedom. Including the plasma's backreaction on the GW makes the energy exchange nonlinear, breaking the usual single-frequency Manley-Rowe picture. For a self-consistent choice of parameters, where backreaction is only a small correction, the unsuppressed resonance still drives the system to a finite-time blow-up within a few Hubble times. We then work out the resonant frequencies and growth rates involved, mapping out what current and future gravitational-wave detectors could observe.

一句话导读：研究早期宇宙磁化手性等离子体中引力波与手性阿尔文波的参数共振及其探测特征。

相关度：low — 本文主要处理经典引力波与手性等离子体的耦合，不涉及全息/AdS/CFT等用户重点方向，因此与当前关注点相关性低。

非最小耦合边界项的PV对称遥平行引力中标量诱导引力波

Scalar induced gravitational waves in PV symmetric teleparallel gravity with a non-minimally coupled boundary term

Fengge Zhang · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：引力相互作用中是否发生宇称破坏的问题近来受到广泛关注。本文研究在包含宇称破坏项和非最小耦合边界项的对称遥平行引力中的标量诱导引力波（SIGWs）。该非最小耦合边界项的存在有助于避免PV对称遥平行引力固有的强耦合问题，从而使得我们能够在此PV对称遥平行引力中研究SIGWs。我们在辐射主导时期数值计算了SIGWs，并利用单色原初功率谱计算了相应的能量密度谱。得到的SIGWs能量密度谱与广义相对论预言相比呈现显著偏差，尤其是在高频段。这一特征可被LISA、天琴、太极等未来空间引力波探测器探测到。

English: The question of whether parity violation occurs in gravitational interactions has recently attracted considerable attention. In this paper, we investigate the scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) in symmetric teleparallel gravity incorporating a parity-violating (PV) term and a non-minimally coupled boundary term. The presence of this non-minimally coupled boundary term helps to avoid the strong coupling problem inherent in PV symmetric teleparallel gravity, thereby enabling our study of SIGWs in this PV symmetric teleparallel gravity. We numerically calculate the SIGWs generated during the radiation-dominated era and compute the corresponding energy density spectrum with a monochromatic primordial power spectrum. The resulting energy density spectrum of SIGWs exhibits significant deviations from the predictions of general relativity, particularly at high frequencies. This distinctive feature is detectable by future space-based gravitational wave detectors like LISA, TianQin, and Taiji.

一句话导读：研究宇称破坏对称遥平行引力中的标量诱导引力波，其能量密度谱与广义相对论预言有显著偏差，可被未来空间引力波探测器探测。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力修正理论和引力波现象，属于用户重点关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线。

Kerr流形上的零流体引力场与Sasaki结构的光学提升

Null fluid gravitational fields on Kerr manifolds and optical lifts of Sasaki structures

Masoud Ganji, Cristina Giannotti, Andrea Spiro · gr-qc, math.DG · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：基于Hill, Lewandowski和Nurowski对适配光学结构的4维洛伦兹度规且满足零流体爱因斯坦方程的刻画，我们在光学结构为Kerr型的假设下给出了该类的显式参数化。直接推论包括：（1）一种构造具有零流体能动张量的爱因斯坦方程解的新方法，产生一大族显式度规，自然包含经典Kerr黑洞度规以及Ganji等人描述的所有Ricci平坦例子；（2）解决了Hill、Lewandowski和Nurowski论文中关于Sasaki CR结构情形光滑光学提升的局部存在性的猜想。

English: Building on the characterisation in [C. D. Hill, J. Lewandowski and P. Nurowski, Indiana Univ. Math. J. 57 (2008), 3131--3176] of 4-dimensional Lorentzian metrics adapted to an optical structure and satisfying the null fluid Einstein equations, we give an explicit parameterisation of this class under the assumption that the optical structure is of Kerr type. As immediate consequences, we obtain: (1) a new method for constructing solutions to the Einstein equations with a null fluid energy momentum tensor, yielding a large family of explicit metrics that naturally includes the classical Kerr black hole metrics and all Ricci flat examples described in [M. Ganji, C. Giannotti, G. Schmalz and A. Spiro, Ann. Physics 75 (2025), Paper No. 169908, 28]; (2) a solution to a conjecture in Hill, Lewandowski and Nurowski's paper on the local existence of smooth optical lifts in the case of Sasaki CR structures.

一句话导读：对Kerr型光学结构的零流体爱因斯坦场方程进行显式参数化，并解决Sasaki情况下光滑光学提升的局部存在性猜想。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力与广义相对论领域，与用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关。

通过旋转大黄蜂时空中的进动和吸积盘图像探测洛伦兹破缺效应

Probing Lorentz-violating effects via precession and accretion disk images in a rotating bumblebee spacetime

Qing Ou, Zhen-Bo Wu, Qian Wan, Peng-Cheng Li · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了旋转大黄蜂时空中强场区域洛伦兹破缺的运动学和光学特征，该时空由标量梯度大黄蜂场产生。对于具有 al≠0 的一般非极端旋转构型，表面 r=r+ 是曲率奇异的，因此在光线追踪计算中现象学地建模为完美吸收内边界。通过分析测试陀螺仪和赤道类时测地线的自转进动，我们发现洛伦兹破缺抑制了静态观测者的 Lense-Thirring 进动，增强了静态球对称极限下的测地进动，并增加了束缚圆轨道的近星点进动频率。几何薄吸积盘图像进一步表明，洛伦兹破缺对临界曲线的影响可忽略，但会缩小与吸收边界相关的内部阴影状特征并增强透镜环。这些结果表明，选定的进动可观测量和外部成像特征可能为强场引力中的洛伦兹破缺效应提供互补诊断，而中心暗特征的形状仍依赖于所采用的内边界方案。

English: We investigate kinematic and optical signatures of Lorentz violation in the strong-field region of a rotating bumblebee spacetime generated by a scalar-gradient bumblebee field. For generic nonextremal rotating configurations with $al\neq0$, the surface $r=r_+$ is curvature singular and is therefore modeled phenomenologically as a perfectly absorbing inner boundary in the ray-tracing calculation. By analyzing the spin precession of test gyroscopes and equatorial timelike geodesics, we find that Lorentz violation suppresses the Lense--Thirring precession of static observers, enhances geodetic precession in the static, spherically symmetric limit, and increases the periastron-precession frequency of bound circular orbits. Images of a geometrically thin accretion disk further show that the Lorentz violation has a negligible impact on the critical curve, while shrinking the inner-shadow-like feature associated with the absorbing boundary and enhancing the lensed ring. These results suggest that selected precession observables and exterior imaging features may provide complementary diagnostics of Lorentz-violating effects in strong-field gravity, whereas the central dark feature remains dependent on the adopted inner-boundary prescription.

一句话导读：研究旋转大黄蜂时空中洛伦兹破缺对自转进动、近星点进动和吸积盘成像的影响，发现某些进动观测和外部成像特征可作为强场引力的补充诊断。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，涉及修改引力理论中的强场可观测效应，与用户重点关注的经典引力方向直接相关。

全局单极时空中有质量量子场的半经典反作用

Semiclassical Backreaction of Massive Quantum Fields in the Spacetime of a Global Monopole

Owen Pavel Fern\'andez Piedra · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了点状全局单极时空中大质量标量场、旋量场和矢量场的领头半经典反作用。从 Schwinger–DeWitt 近似下得到的重整化应力-能量张量出发，我们推导由一般守恒对角源产生的一阶半经典几何，然后专门讨论自旋 0、1/2 和 1 的场。局域固定的量子源按 r^{-6} 衰减，并诱导 r^{-4} 量级的度规修正。标量场通常产生不同的时间与径向度规函数，而旋量场和矢量场导致单函数几何。我们分析了由此产生的曲率修正、静态观测者的加速度、测地运动以及一阶解的适用范围。量子反作用对单极外部产生局部形变，但不改变其渐近立体角亏损。

English: We study the leading semiclassical backreaction of massive scalar, spinor, and vector fields in the spacetime of a pointlike global monopole. Starting from the renormalized stress-energy tensors obtained in the Schwinger--DeWitt approximation, we derive the first-order semiclassical geometry generated by a general conserved diagonal source and then specialize it to fields of spin $0$, $1/2$, and $1$. The locally fixed quantum source falls as $r^{-6}$ and induces metric corrections of order $r^{-4}$. The scalar field generically produces distinct temporal and radial metric functions, whereas the spinor and vector fields lead to one-function geometries. We analyze the resulting curvature correction, the acceleration of static observers, geodesic motion, and the validity domain of the first-order solution. The quantum backreaction produces a local deformation of the monopole exterior but does not modify its asymptotic solid-angle deficit.

一句话导读：利用 Schwinger–DeWitt 重整化应力张量，推导全局单极时空中大质量场的领头半经典反作用：量子源按 r^{-6} 衰减并在 r^{-4} 量级局部改变度规，但渐近立体角亏损不变。

相关度：medium — 该文属于半经典引力/广义相对论方向，符合用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”，但与全息/强关联等主线无关；因此给出中等相关。

偏心率调制的相位简并性与EMRI中暗物质与吸积盘环境效应的可区分性

Eccentricity-Modulated Phase Degeneracy and Distinguishability between Dark Matter and Accretion Disk Environmental Effects in EMRIs

Tian-hao Wu, Shu-Jun Rong · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：极端质量比旋进（EMRI）是探测大质量黑洞周围弱环境效应的灵敏探针，因为这些效应可以积累成可观测的引力波相位移动。本文研究了偏心EMRI波形中暗物质晕与吸积盘之间的相位简并性。我们用NFW和Beta晕轮廓对暗物质（DM）环境进行建模，并用薄α盘模型描述吸积盘。通过依赖于偏心率的相位诊断和LISA频带中的剩余信噪比（SNR）来量化它们的可区分性。结果表明，DM引起的退相位对初始偏心率e0的依赖较弱，而盘引起的退相位随e0增大被强烈抑制。可区分时间对初始偏心率呈现平滑且弱非单调的依赖，同时更强烈地受环境强度控制。对于本文考虑的基准系统，DM与吸积盘波形差异可被LISA探测到，且e0可作为除观测时长之外的辅助诊断量。

English: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) are sensitive probes of weak environmental effects around massive black holes, since such effects can accumulate into observable gravitational-wave phase shifts. In this work, we study the phase degeneracy between dark matter halos and accretion disks in eccentric EMRI waveforms. We model the dark matter (DM) environment with NFW and Beta halo profiles, and describe the disk using a thin $\alpha$-disk model. Their distinguishability is quantified through eccentricity-dependent phase diagnostics and the residual signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) in the LISA band. Our results show that DM-induced dephasing depends only weakly on the initial eccentricity $e_0$, whereas disk-induced dephasing is strongly suppressed as $e_0$ increases. The distinguishability time exhibits a smooth and weakly non-monotonic dependence on the initial eccentricity, while being more strongly controlled by the environmental strength. For the benchmark systems considered here, the DM--disk waveform difference can be detectable by LISA, and $e_0$ can serve as an auxiliary diagnostic in addition to the observation duration.

一句话导读：研究偏心EMRI中暗物质晕与吸积盘引起的引力波退相位简并性，发现二者可区分性随初始偏心率变化，LISA可探测基准系统差异。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，研究引力波天体环境效应，与全息/量子引力主线无直接关联。

随机鸣响：利用天体物理引力波背景测量哈勃常数

The Stochastic Siren: Astrophysical Gravitational-Wave Background Measurements of the Hubble Constant

Bryce Cousins, Kristen Schumacher, Adrian Ka-Wai Chung, Colm Talbot, Thomas Callister, Daniel E. Holz, Nicol\'as Yunes · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们报道了首次利用双黑洞并合产生的随机引力波背景测量哈勃常数 H0。该天体物理背景对宇宙膨胀历史敏感，因此可以独立于电磁方法以及引力波标准汽笛方法用于宇宙学参数推断。我们描述了该背景的宇宙学依赖性，并展示如何将其用作“随机汽笛”来测量 H0。通过分析现有的已解析双黑洞并合以及当前对背景的非探测，我们发现相对于仅使用已解析并合，H0 可以更精确地被测量。我们还注意到，随机汽笛可能在哈勃张力中发挥独特作用，因为 H0 测量的下限会随着对背景的持续非探测而逐渐增加。

English: We report the first measurement of the Hubble constant $H_0$ using the stochastic gravitational-wave background arising from binary black hole mergers. This astrophysical background is sensitive to the expansion history of the Universe and thus can be used for cosmological parameter inference independently of not only electromagnetic methods, but also gravitational-wave standard siren approaches. We describe the background's cosmological dependence and show how it can be used as a ``stochastic siren'' to measure $H_0$. By analyzing existing resolved binary black hole mergers and the current non-detection of the background, we find that $H_0$ can be measured more accurately relative to using resolved mergers alone. We also note that the stochastic siren may serve a unique role in the Hubble tension in that the lower bound of the $H_0$ measurement would progressively increase with continued non-detection of the background.

一句话导读：利用随机引力波背景作为“随机汽笛”来测量哈勃常数，并表明其比单独使用并合事件更精确，且对哈勃张力有独特意义。

相关度：low — 论文不涉及全息原理或广义相对论理论，而是引力波天文学和宇宙学参数测量，与用户关注的理论物理方向关联较低。

暗物质环境对黑洞阴影的微扰效应

Perturbative Effects of Dark Matter Environments on Black Hole Shadows

Gabriel Gomez · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：构建描述嵌入暗物质环境中的黑洞时空解，是在强引力区域探测暗物质性质的关键一步。然而，目前尚无统一或系统的方法来模拟这类构型，且几种常用方法存在方法论上的模糊性。基于这些挑战，我们建立了一个微扰框架来描述由周围暗物质分布引起的静态球对称黑洞的形变。在该框架内，我们计算了光子球半径和黑洞阴影半径的首阶修正，并假设了一个通用的暗物质晕轮廓。随后，我们将该形式应用于具有物理意义的密度轮廓，包括 Hernquist 和 Navarro-Frenk-White 模型，得到了 Schwarzschild 背景下微扰度规函数和临界碰撞参数的闭式解析表达式。利用这些结果，我们获得了相应的阴影偏差的定量估计，发现它们远超出 Keck 和 VLTI 测量所设定的当前观测界限。作为一致性检查，我们进一步估算了 S2 星轨道半径内包围的暗物质总质量，并表明其远低于 GRAVITY 合作组报告的0.1%上限。总的来说，该方法为研究暗物质对黑洞现象学的微扰效应（包括对引力波观测的潜在影响）提供了一条系统途径。

English: Constructing spacetime solutions that describe black holes embedded in dark matter environments is a crucial step toward probing the properties of dark matter in the strong-field regime of gravity. At present, however, there is no unique or systematic framework to model such configurations, and several commonly adopted approaches raise methodological ambiguities. Motivated by these challenges, we build upon a perturbative framework to describe deformations of static, spherically symmetric black holes induced by a surrounding dark matter distribution. Within this framework, we compute the leading-order corrections to both the photon-sphere radius and the radius of the black hole shadow, assuming a generic dark matter halo profile. We then apply the formalism to physically motivated density profiles, including the Hernquist and Navarro-Frenk-White models, obtaining closed-form analytical expressions for the perturbed metric functions and for the critical impact parameter in the Schwarzschild background. Using these results, we obtain quantitative estimates for the corresponding shadow deviations and find that they lie well beyond the current observational bounds set by Keck and VLTI measurements. As a consistency check, we further estimate the total dark matter mass enclosed within the orbital radius of the S2 star and show that it remains well below the $0.1\%$ upper limit reported by the GRAVITY collaboration. Overall, this approach offers a systematic avenue to investigate perturbative effects of dark matter on black hole phenomenology, including potential implications for gravitational wave observations.

一句话导读：建立微扰框架计算暗物质环境对黑洞阴影半径的修正，并给出解析结果与观测比较。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论领域，与用户重点标签中的经典引力支线相关，但不涉及全息或凝聚态等方向。

翘曲盘与极端质量比旋进碰撞产生的准周期喷发：亮度-重现图与引力波后续观测

QPEs from Warped Disk Collisions with EMRIs: Brightness-Recurrence Diagram and Gravitational-Wave Follow-up

Bo-An Chen, Bei You, Giovanni Miniutti, Ning Jiang, Zhen Pan, Tao Yang, Xi-Long Fan, Kai Liao, Xu-Heng Ding, Zong-Hong Zhu, Shuai-Kang Yang, Sai-En Xu, Han He, Xiao Fan · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.GA, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：准周期喷发（QPEs）显示出相关的长/短和强/弱模式，现有平盘碰撞模型无法解释。我们提出这些特征源于极端质量比旋进（EMRI）与翘曲吸积盘碰撞，该盘可能在潮汐瓦解事件后形成。翘曲同时调制重现时间和爆发能量，将盘几何结构——从而中心超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的自旋——编码进X射线光变曲线。我们引入亮度-重现图（BRD）来可视化这种相关性，其中QPE爆发沿由EMRI近星点进动驱动的椭圆轨迹运行；该椭圆的倾斜编码了EMRI相对于SMBH自旋是顺行还是逆行。将该模型应用于典型QPE源GSN 069，成功再现了观测到的模式。数据与顺行恒星伴星或逆行恒星级黑洞一致。在恒星级黑洞情形下，持续的轨道衰减可能使EMRI在未来几十年内被LISA探测到，从而促进引力波后续观测和对系统的独立多信使约束。

English: Quasi-Periodic Eruptions (QPEs) display correlated long/short and strong/weak patterns that remain unexplained by existing flat-disk collision models. We propose that these features arise from an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) colliding with a warped accretion disk, likely formed after a tidal disruption event. The warp modulates both recurrence time and burst energy, encoding the disk geometry -- and thus the spin of the central supermassive black hole (SMBH) -- into the X-ray light curve. We introduce the Brightness-Recurrence Diagram (BRD) to visualize this correlation, where QPE bursts trace an elliptical trajectory driven by the EMRI's apsidal precession; the tilt of this ellipse encodes whether the EMRI is prograde or retrograde relative to the SMBH spin. Applying this model to the prototypical QPE source GSN 069 successfully reproduces the observed patterns. The data are consistent with either a prograde stellar secondary or a retrograde stellar-mass black hole. In the stellar-mass black hole scenario, ongoing orbital decay could render the EMRI detectable by LISA within a few decades, facilitating gravitational-wave follow-up and independent multimessenger constraints on the system.

一句话导读：提出EMRI与翘曲吸积盘碰撞模型，解释QPE长/短与强/弱相关模式，并预测LISA引力波后续探测。

相关度：medium — 论文研究EMRI轨道动力学、翘曲吸积盘碰撞和引力波探测，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，涉及黑洞与引力波现象，但不是全息理论。

弱引力透镜的统计场论

Statistical Field Theory for Weak Gravitational Lensing

Zheng Zhang, Philip Bull, Chris Clarkson, Andrina Nicola · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：标准弱透镜计算将透镜视为沿视线对物质场的线性重映射。我们转而将透镜化为Sachs光学标量的随机场论，由随机Ricci聚焦和Weyl剪切场驱动。所得的路径积分生成透镜观测量任意n点关联函数的图解展开，组织为线性响应、非线性传播和驱动场累积量。常规计算作为最低阶线性传播极限出现。在其之上，非线性Sachs演化与驱动场非高斯性耦合，将物质累积量层级混合到透镜层级中。一个选择规则控制耦合：n点观测量直接接收n点驱动场累积量的贡献，其领先层级混合修正来自(n+1)点累积量通过一次非线性Sachs相互作用，更高累积量仅在更高阶进入。例如，两点函数被Ricci聚焦和Weyl剪切的三点累积量修正，使小尺度模式跨尺度馈入透镜信号，并以相当比例填充E模和B模。该形式不是一个限制性近似方案，而是一个范式转变：一个统一框架自然容纳路径修正、高阶物质统计、随机性和小尺度效应。

English: Standard weak-lensing calculations treat lensing as a linear remapping of the matter field along the line of sight. We instead formulate lensing as a stochastic field theory for the Sachs optical scalars, driven by random Ricci-focusing and Weyl-shearing fields. The resulting path integral generates a diagrammatic expansion for arbitrary $n$-point correlation functions of lensing observables, organised into linear response, nonlinear propagation, and driving-field cumulants. The conventional calculation emerges as the lowest-order, linear-propagation limit. Beyond it, nonlinear Sachs evolution couples to driving-field non-Gaussianity, mixing the matter cumulant hierarchy into the lensing hierarchy. A selection rule governs the couplings: an $n$-point observable receives a direct contribution from the $n$-point driving-field cumulant, and its leading hierarchy-mixing correction from the $(n+1)$-point cumulant via one nonlinear Sachs interaction, with higher cumulants entering only at higher order. The two-point function, for instance, is corrected by three-point cumulants of Ricci focusing and Weyl shearing, letting small-scale modes feed the lensing signal across scales and populating the $E$- and $B$-modes in comparable measure. Rather than a restrictive approximation scheme, the formalism is a paradigm shift: a unified framework naturally accommodating path corrections, higher-order matter statistics, stochasticity, and small-scale effects.

一句话导读：将弱引力透镜构造为Sachs光学标量的随机场论，用路径积分统一处理非线性传播与高阶物质统计。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论领域，是用户重点关注支线之一，且提出新的统计场论框架，关联度高。

利用强透镜LISA引力波的波光学畸变探测暗物质次结构

Probing Dark Matter Substructure with Wave-Optics Distortions of Strongly Lensed LISA Gravitational Waves

Tonghua Liu, Kai Liao, Marek Biesiada, Jieci Wang · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：强透镜效应除了改变引力波信号的幅度和到达时间外，还会改变其相位。本文研究这种相位信息能否区分透镜中的三种暗物质结构：Navarro–Frenk–White晕（NFW）、自相互作用暗物质晕（SIDM）和模糊暗物质场（FDM）。作者为四年的LISA任务中可探测的透镜化大质量黑洞双星族生成波形，并用带有外部剪切的平滑奇异等温椭球透镜对每个信号进行拟合。在132个透镜系统中，311个像在时间上被分辨为独立信号。NFW和SIDM会产生真实的波形变化，但其缓变部分在很大程度上与平滑Fermat势在像点处的常数、梯度和Hessian简并。FDM的相干密度涨落在拟合后留下更大的频率相关残差。NFW–FDM和SIDM–FDM族分别约需60和110个分辨像波形即可区分。这些结果表明，重复的透镜化LISA信号可以探测透镜星系中暗物质的空间形态，而不仅仅是总透镜质量。

English: Strong lensing changes the phase of a gravitational-wave signal as well as its amplitude and arrival time. We study whether this phase information can distinguish between three dark-matter structures in the lens: a Navarro--Frenk--White halo (NFW), a self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) halo, and a fuzzy-dark-matter field (FDM). We generate waveforms for the detectable lensed massive-black-hole-binary population of a four-year LISA mission and fit every signal with the same smooth singular-isothermal-ellipsoid lens with external shear. In 132 lens systems, 311 images are resolved as separate signals in time. NFW and SIDM produce real waveform changes, but their slowly varying part is largely degenerate with the constant, gradient, and Hessian of the smooth Fermat potential at the image. The coherent density fluctuations of FDM leave a larger frequency-dependent residual after this fit. The NFW--FDM and SIDM--FDM populations become distinguishable with about 60 and 110 resolved image waveforms, respectively. These results show that repeated lensed LISA signals can probe the spatial form of dark matter in lens galaxies, rather than only the total lensing mass.

一句话导读：通过强透镜LISA引力波信号的波光学相位畸变，区分透镜中不同暗物质结构（NFW、SIDM、FDM）。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论框架下的引力波透镜与暗物质研究，与用户重点关注的全息/量子引力方向不同，但与经典引力支线相关。
