重塑引力：广义对称性、双拷贝与旋转黑洞

Reimagining Gravity: Generalized Symmetries, Double Copy, and Spinning Black Holes

Joon-Hwi Kim · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：广义相对论已有一百年历史。然而，通过广义对称性、散射振幅和有效场论的现代探索提出了若干开放问题，促使人们重新评估关于引力、时空和自旋的传统观点。第一，广义对称性是否作为低能EFT存在于动力学引力中？第二，广义相对论与杨-米尔斯理论之间的树级双拷贝关系是否存在场论解释？第三，在点粒子有效理论中，四维旋转黑洞在外部场中的精确运动方程或拉格朗日量是什么？本论文从三个不同视角研究引力，以阐明这些问题。第一部分将引力视为洛伦兹群的规范理论，建立动力学引力的一形式对称性。第二部分探讨引力在多大程度上可视为微分同胚的规范理论，联系颜色-运动学对偶与双拷贝。第三部分将四维引力视为自对偶与反自对偶部分之间的非线性相互作用，并应用此视角推导四维旋转黑洞解及其在点粒子有效理论中的动力学。首先，我们从经典双拷贝导出新的自对偶黑洞度规，并证明Kerr度规代表一对自对偶和反自对偶Taub-NUT解，将Newman-Janis算法提升为严格推导。其次，我们提出Newman-Janis算法的动力学探针对应物，给出了旋转黑洞Compton振幅的拉格朗日推导，该振幅对所有螺旋度构型具有正确的因子化且无虚假极点，同时发展了手性形式主义，以体现并最大限度利用自对偶扇区的简单性。

English: General relativity is a century-old subject. Yet modern explorations through generalized symmetries, scattering amplitudes, and effective field theory have raised open problems, motivating a reassessment of conventional views on gravitation, spacetime, and spin. First, do generalized symmetries exist in dynamical gravity as a low-energy EFT? Second, is there a field-theoretic explanation for the tree-level double copy relationship between general relativity and Yang-Mills theory? Third, what are the exact equations of motion or Lagrangians of four-dimensional spinning black holes in external fields, in point-particle effective theory? In this dissertation, three different perspectives on gravity are developed to shed light on each of these puzzles. In Part I, we view gravity as a gauge theory of Lorentz group to establish a one-form symmetry of dynamical gravity. In Part II, we investigate how far gravity can be viewed as a gauge theory of diffeomorphisms, in connection with color-kinematics duality and double copy. In Part III, we view four-dimensional gravity as a nonlinear interaction between self-dual and anti-self-dual parts. We apply this view to the derivation of four-dimensional spinning black hole solutions as well as their dynamics in point-particle effective theory. First, we derive a new self-dual black hole metric from classical double copy and show that the Kerr metric represents a pair of self-dual and anti-self-dual Taub-NUT solutions, elevating the Newman-Janis algorithm to a rigorous derivation. Second, we propose a dynamical probe counterpart of Newman-Janis algorithm. We give a Lagrangian derivation of spinning black hole Compton amplitudes that exhibit correct factorizations and are free of spurious poles for all helicity configurations while developing a chiral formalism that manifests and maximally utilizes the simplicity of the self-dual sector.

一句话导读：本文从广义对称性、双拷贝和自对偶结构三个视角重新审视引力，推导旋转黑洞解与Compton振幅。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究经典引力、双拷贝与旋转黑洞，与用户聚焦的经典引力/广义相对论相关，但未涉及全息或凝聚态方向，关联度中等。

Carroll-Cotton张量与零无穷远处的引力辐射

Carroll-Cotton Tensors and Gravitational Radiation at Null Infinity

Adrien Fiorucci, Simon Pekar, P. Marios Petropoulos, Matthieu Vilatte · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们通过对共形Carroll代数进行规范，提供了共形Carroll几何上Cotton张量的第一性原理定义。在数学方面，这一分析产生了一个共形协变对象，旨在量化Carroll几何中偏离共形平坦性的程度。在物理方面，它为描述四维渐近平直时空共形边界处的引力辐射自由度提供了一个强大的几何工具。与Bondi新闻不同，其完全协变的定义需要由我们认定为Geroch张量的补充数据来修正，而Carroll-Cotton张量则提供了自然的几何对象来量化因引力波发射而导致的非平稳性偏离。

English: We provide a first-principles definition of Cotton tensors on conformal Carroll geometries by gauging the conformal Carroll algebra. On the mathematical side, this analysis yields a conformally covariant object designed to quantify deviations from conformal flatness in Carrollian geometries. On the physical side, it offers a powerful geometric tool to describe gravitational radiative degrees of freedom at the conformal boundary of four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Unlike the Bondi news, whose fully-covariant definition requires amendment by supplementary data that we identify with the Geroch tensor, the Carroll-Cotton tensors offer the natural geometric objects to quantify deviations from stationarity due to the emission of gravitational waves.

一句话导读：该论文通过规范共形Carroll代数定义Carroll-Cotton张量，为描述渐近平直时空零无穷远处的引力辐射自由度提供了新的几何工具。

来自AdS$_3$上广义相对论的全息warped共形场论

Holographic warped CFTs from General Relativity on AdS$_3$

Kristiansen Lara, Ricardo Troncoso · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们为具有负宇宙学常数的三维爱因斯坦引力引入一组新的边界条件。规范生成元以标准形式清晰地实现了Virasoro与u(1) Kac-Moody代数的半直和，无需对整体电荷进行额外重定义。我们证明，要求欧几里得BTZ和热AdS$_3$互为微分同胚恰好诱导出化学势的warped模S变换。相应的warped Cardy公式精确重现了Bekenstein-Hawking熵，其中基态被识别为欧几里得AdS$_3$。此外，我们还与Brown-Henneaux和Compere-Song-Strominger的边界条件进行了明确比较。

English: We introduce a new set of boundary conditions for three-dimensional Einstein gravity with negative cosmological constant. The canonical generators plainly realize the semidirect sum of Virasoro and $u(1)$ Kac-Moody algebras in its standard form, without the need of additional redefinitions of the global charges. We show that requiring Euclidean BTZ and thermal AdS$_3$ to be diffeomorphic precisely induces the warped modular $S$-transformation of the chemical potentials. The corresponding warped Cardy formula then exactly reproduces the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, where the ground state is identified as Euclidean AdS$_3$. An explicit comparison with the boundary conditions of Brown-Henneaux and Compere-Song-Strominger is also addressed.

一句话导读：通过AdS3引力新边界条件实现warped CFT，其warped Cardy公式精确给出黑熵。

模流化纠缠熵中的不等式与正性

Inequalities and Positivity in Modular Flowed Entanglement Entropy

Liangyu Chen · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：模哈密顿量在量子信息理论、量子场论和AdS/CFT对应中扮演重要角色。本文研究一个特定的动力学设定：从参考态的约化密度矩阵的张量积出发，在该参考态自身生成的模流下演化这个乘积态。然后我们考察给定区域纠缠熵的相应变化，称之为模纠缠位移（MES）。与固定量子信道下的演化不同，这种模演化不遵循要求MES具有确定符号的一般原理。对于分成子系统A和B的二分割，我们建立了一个精确的熵平衡：A和B的MES之和等于沿轨道产生的互信息。因此，它们的和是非负的，尽管单个位移可能为负。然而，对于二维共形场论真空中的两个不相交区间，我们证明交换这两个区间的全局共形对合强制两个MES相等。由此可知，每个MES沿模流是非负的。我们既通过共形输运的截断方式，又内在地利用Araki相对熵建立了这一结果。最后，我们将模流轨道等同于Connes cocycle轨道，并讨论其在AdS/CFT对应中的可能应用。

English: The modular Hamiltonian plays an important role in quantum information theory, quantum field theory, and the AdS/CFT correspondence. In this paper, we study a specific dynamical setup: starting from the tensor product of the reduced density matrices of a reference state, we evolve this product state under the modular flow generated by the reference state itself. We then investigate the resulting change in the entanglement entropy of a given region, which we call the modular entanglement shift (MES). Unlike evolution under a fixed quantum channel, this modular evolution obeys no general principle requiring the MES to have a definite sign. For a bipartition into subsystems A and B, we establish an exact entropy balance: the sum of the MESs for A and B equals the mutual information generated along the orbit. Consequently, their sum is nonnegative, even though either individual shift may be negative. For two disjoint intervals in the vacuum of a two-dimensional conformal field theory, however, we prove that a global conformal involution exchanging the intervals forces the two MESs to be equal. It follows that each MES is nonnegative along the modular flow. We establish this result both using a conformally transported regulator and intrinsically in terms of Araki relative entropy. Finally, we identify the modular-flow orbit with a Connes cocycle orbit and discuss possible applications to the AdS/CFT correspondence.

一句话导读：研究了模流演化下纠缠熵的变化（模纠缠位移），证明了二分割的熵平衡不等式，并在二维CFT双区间情形下证明了每个MES的非负性，联系到Connes cocycle轨道与AdS/CFT。

相关度：low — 该论文属于量子信息与AdS/CFT基础理论，与用户重点关注的超流、相变、QNM等应用方向无直接关联，因而相关性低。

超越最小 type-A 的高自旋 dS/CFT，以及 de Sitter 空间的双重本性

Higher-Spin dS/CFT beyond minimal type-A, and the doubled nature of de Sitter space

Julian Lang, Yasha Neiman · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了自由矢量模型与四维 de Sitter 空间中高自旋引力之间的全息对偶。在体态和边界两侧，我们引入了新的现实条件，使得我们能够在体态中包含奇数自旋，并将对偶从 type-A 扩展到 type-B（即从边界标量扩展到旋量）。新的现实条件与 de Sitter 空间中单粒子（超）多重态的高自旋对称希尔伯特空间范数密切相关。我们构建了关于 type-A 和 type-B 理论的两种替代视角。一种视角中，两者共存于“平行宇宙”中，具体表现为 de Sitter 空间的偶/奇对映体同一化。另一种视角中，type-A 和 type-B 是同一扭空间理论在欧几里得 AdS 中的勒让德变换描述（这涉及到高自旋配分函数与其自身勒让德对偶的观察）。以此方式，高自旋全息调和了 de Sitter 空间的双边界本性与欧几里得 AdS 的单边界本性。在适当的现实条件下，统一 type-A 和 type-B 以及体态费米子的超对称理论无法保持一致。

English: We study the holographic duality between free vector models and Higher-Spin Gravity in 4-dimensional de Sitter space. On both the bulk and boundary sides, we introduce new reality conditions, which allow us to include odd spins in the bulk, and to extend the duality from type-A to type-B (i.e. from boundary scalars to spinors). The new reality conditions are closely related to a higher-spin-symmetric Hilbert-space norm for the single-particle (super)multiplet in de Sitter space. We construct two alternative viewpoints on the type-A and type-B theories. In one view, the two co-exist in "parallel universes", specifically in even vs. odd antipodal identifications of de Sitter space. In the other view, type-A and type-B are Legendre-transformed descriptions of the same twistor-space theory in Euclidean AdS (this involves the observation that the higher-spin partition function is Legendre-dual to itself). In this way, higher-spin holography reconciles the two-boundary nature of de Sitter space with the one-boundary nature of Euclidean AdS. The supersymmetric theory that unifies type-A and type-B with bulk fermions fails to be consistent under the appropriate reality conditions.

一句话导读：研究自由矢量模型与四维 de Sitter 空间中高自旋引力的全息对偶，通过新的现实条件扩展至 type-B，并揭示 de Sitter 空间的双重本性。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦高自旋 dS/CFT 与 type-A/type-B 扩展，与用户重点标签（全息超流超导、p波、相变、QNM等）不直接相关，但可能对全息框架的背景有间接参考意义。

潜伏几何与矩阵量子力学中洛伦兹时间的涌现

Latent Geometry and the Emergence of Lorentzian Time in Matrix Quantum Mechanics

Badis Ydri · hep-th, gr-qc, hep-lat, hep-ph, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们在BFSS/BMN模型中引入“潜伏几何”（latent geometry）。在几何转变中涌现几何蒸发之前，大$d$高斯化将其信息编码在由baby模糊球选择的特殊$N=2$扇区中，即一个自旋$1/2$的Pauli块。维度约化、有限$N$高斯化和精确的Molien-Weyl投影将此编码传递到质量变形的BFSS$_2$。在$N=2$时，同一稳定的BFSS$_2$振子允许不等价的幺正$\mathbf{H}^2_\theta=\mathbf{EAdS}^2_\theta$和$\mathbf{dS}^2_\theta$解码，实现时空嬗变和洛伦兹时间的涌现。

English: We introduce \emph{latent geometry} in BFSS/BMN models. Before an emergent geometry evaporates at the geometric transition, large-$d$ Gaussianization encodes its information in the exceptional $N=2$ sector selected by the baby fuzzy sphere, a single active spin-$1/2$ Pauli block. Dimensional reduction, finite-$N$ Gaussianization and exact Molien--Weyl projection transport this encoding to mass-deformed BFSS$_2$. At $N=2$, the same stable BFSS$_2$ oscillator admits inequivalent unitary $\mathbf{H}^2_\theta=\mathbf{EAdS}^2_\theta$ and $\mathbf{dS}^2_\theta$ decodings, realizing spacetime transmutation and the emergence of Lorentzian time.

一句话导读：在BFSS/BMN矩阵量子力学中提出“潜伏几何”机制，通过N=2扇区实现EAdS2与dS2解码的时空嬗变，解释洛伦兹时间的涌现。

BMN 传播复杂度跨相变行为

BMN Spread Complexity Across Phase Transitions

Dibakar Roychowdhury · hep-th, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究质量变形 BMN 矩阵模型中量子态的动力学行为与传播复杂度，该模型在有限温度 β^-1 和化学势 ν 下具有涌现的 U(1) 全局电荷。在强耦合区（μ ≫ 1），我们通过带电热场双态（cTFD）建模动力学，并追踪传播复杂度在三个不同热力学区域的演化。在低温有隙相（βμ ≫ 1），离散质量隙束缚态占主导，抑制波包色散并产生非混沌的振荡性早期增长。相反，在高温连续相（βμ ≪ 1），热激发淹没质量隙，驱动向连续平流场转变，表现出最大混沌扰乱，其 Krylov Lyapunov 指数 λ_K = π/β 饱和普适界限。在中间温度区（βμ ∼ 1, βν ∼ 1），质量隙束缚态与连续热背景的相互作用导致 Lanczos 系数出现次领头修正 b_n ∼ (π/β)n + γ√n，支配完全混沌热化之前的连续次指数交叉。关于 KMS 边界条件、巨正则谱矩、留数分析以及 Krylov 空间中时间反演对称性破缺的技术推导详见四个附录。

English: We investigate the dynamical behavior and spread complexity of quantum states within the mass-deformed BMN matrix model equipped with an emergent $U(1)$ global charge at finite temperature $\beta^{-1}$ and chemical potential $\nu$. Focusing on the strong-coupling regime ($\mu \gg 1$), we model the dynamics via a charged Thermofield Double (cTFD) state and trace the evolution of spread complexity across three distinct thermodynamic regimes. In the low-temperature gapped phase ($\beta \mu \gg 1$), discrete mass-gap bound states dominate, trapping wavepacket dispersion and producing non-chaotic, oscillatory early-time growth. Conversely, in the high-temperature continuum phase ($\beta \mu \ll 1$), thermal excitations overwhelm the mass gap, driving a transition to a continuous advection field that exhibits maximal chaotic scrambling with a Krylov Lyapunov exponent $\lambda_K = \pi / \beta$ that saturates the universal bound. In the intermediate temperature regime ($\beta \mu \sim 1, \beta \nu \sim 1$), the interplay between mass-gap bound states and the continuous thermal background induces a sub-leading correction to the Lanczos coefficients $b_n \sim \frac{\pi}{\beta} n + \gamma \sqrt{n}$, governing a continuous sub-exponential crossover before full chaotic thermalization. Technical derivations regarding KMS boundary conditions, grand canonical spectral moments, residue analysis, and time-reversal symmetry breaking in Krylov space are detailed in four dedicated appendices.

一句话导读：研究强耦合 BMN 矩阵模型中有 U(1) 电荷的 cTFD 态的传播复杂度，揭示低温非混沌振荡与高温 Krylov 混沌界饱和的相变行为。

相关度：low — 论文属于 BMN 矩阵模型与量子混沌/复杂度方向，与用户重点标签中的全息超流、探针膜、冷原子、经典引力等无直接关联；仅在相变/混沌概念上有弱相似，故标记为低相关。

超对称D=11近地平线几何的warp因子：单点刚性、Spin(7)完全平方与全局约束

The warp factor of supersymmetric $D=11$ near-horizon geometries: single-point rigidity, the $Spin(7)$ perfect square, and global constraints

Usman Kayani · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在紧致连通的截面S上，超对称M-视界的Killing旋量双线性给出了一个点态恒等式，将warp因子Δ、旋转一形式h和两个旋量范数联系起来，不假设Killing旋量的范数。我们推导该未约化恒等式的三个推论。第一，单点刚性定理：若Δ和h在截面某一点消失，则通量恒为零，S为Ricci平坦，视界为R^{1,1}×S。一个单点取代了通常的整体旋量假设。第二，通量的Spin(7)分解代数地固定了h，并展示了已知在适配规范中的标量平方Δ=4Φ²的不变通量内容，即Δ=(1/108)||w_7 ± σ(Y_7)||²。只有两个7求和项w_7、Y_7达到warp因子，且该平方是退化的而非定的：它在线性子空间w_7 = ∓σ(Y_7)上消失而非在原点，因此Δ的正性不产生情形列表。第三，两者结合成点态预算：超对称的单一常数在偏离静态性和通量的Spin(7)失配之间共享，每一项都以该常数为界。同样的恒等式刻画了常值性假设（等价于双线性一形式V的V=-fh），该假设在已知翘曲AdS_2解的静态分支上失败；它们固定了warp因子的加权积分；并将视界的Komar角动量化为正的双线性积分。文中陈述了两个否定性命题（关于由Killing旋量构造的隐藏对称性和由其双线性构造的第二个等度规），并给出了其假设。

English: On a compact connected section ${\cal S}$ of a supersymmetric $M$-horizon the Killing-spinor bilinears give a pointwise identity relating the warp factor $\Delta$, the rotation one-form $h$ and the two spinor norms, with nothing assumed about the norm of the Killing spinor. We derive three consequences of that unreduced identity. First, a single-point rigidity theorem: if $\Delta$ and $h$ vanish at one point of the section, then the flux vanishes identically, ${\cal S}$ is Ricci-flat and the horizon is $\mathbb{R}^{1,1}\times{\cal S}$. A single point replaces the usual global spinorial hypothesis. Second, the ${\rm Spin}(7)$ decomposition of the flux fixes $h$ algebraically and exhibits the invariant flux content of the scalar square $\Delta=4\Phi^2$ known in an adapted gauge, $\Delta=\frac{1}{108}\|w_{\bf 7}\pm\sigma({\cal Y}_{\bf 7})\|^2$. Only the two ${\bf 7}$-summands $w_{\bf 7}$, ${\cal Y}_{\bf 7}$ reach the warp factor, and the square is degenerate rather than definite: it vanishes on the linear subspace $w_{\bf 7}=\mp\sigma({\cal Y}_{\bf 7})$ rather than at the origin, so positivity of $\Delta$ yields no case list. Third, the two combine into a pointwise budget: the single constant of supersymmetry is shared between the deviation from staticity and the ${\rm Spin}(7)$ mismatch of the flux, each bounded by that constant. The same identities characterise the constancy hypothesis, equivalent to $V=-fh$ for the bilinear one-form $V$, which fails on the static branch of known warped $AdS_2$ solutions; they fix a weighted integral of the warp factor; and they reduce the Komar angular momentum of the horizon to a positive bilinear integral. Two no-go statements, for hidden symmetries built from the Killing spinor and for a second isometry from its bilinears, are stated with their hypotheses.

一句话导读：本文证明D=11超对称近地平线几何中warp因子的单点刚性定理，并推导其Spin(7)分解与全局约束。

相关度：medium — 论文研究D=11超引力近地平线几何的warp因子刚性，属于经典超引力/广义相对论，与用户关注的经典引力分支相关，但未涉及全息或具体凝聚态物理。

从$T\bar T$流导出的膜有效作用及其island规则

Brane effective actions and their island rule from $T\overline T$ flows

Nele Callebaut, Matteo Selle · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们通过在导数展开中求解径向哈密顿流，导出了具有任意张力EOW膜的半经典AdS$_3$引力的二维有效作用量。将径向流解释为重正化群流后，该有效理论被认同为类$T\bar T$形变的CFT。由此我们提出，具有Neumann或共形边界条件的半经典AdS$_3$引力的全息对偶可通过令该类$T\bar T$形变CFT自由而得到。我们针对空AdS$_3$中$(A)dS_2$膜对应的鞍点明确验证了这一提议。我们讨论了将有效膜理论分解为物质部分（分别为$T\bar T$形变、类$T\bar T$形变和未形变的CFT）与相应膜世界引力部分的三种有用方式。特别地，将$T\bar T$形变CFT与类$T\bar T$形变Liouville引力耦合的分解直接把积分Weyl反常的Liouville描述推广到体内部。随后，该有效作用量被用于在AdS/bCFT设置中对非渐近膜进行island规则计算，发现与Ryu-Takayanagi结果精确一致。这将island规则的既有全息检验扩展到非渐近适用区域，并为$T\bar T$纠缠提供了Wald熵解释。最后，我们通过显式计算壳上作用量，将有效理论及其高阶导数修正与具有涨落径向截断的AdS$_3$引力联系起来。

English: We derive a $2d$ effective action for semi-classical AdS$_3$ gravity with an EOW brane of arbitrary tension by solving the radial Hamiltonian flow in a derivative expansion. Interpreting the radial flow as an RG flow, the effective theory is identified with a $T\bar T$-like deformed CFT. This leads us to propose that the holographic dual for semi-classical AdS$_3$ gravity with Neumann or conformal boundary conditions is obtained by setting the $T\bar T$-like deformed CFT free. We verify our proposal explicitly for saddles corresponding to $(A)dS_2$ branes in empty AdS$_3$. We discuss three useful ways to decompose the effective brane theory into a matter sector ($T\bar T$-deformed, $T\bar T$-like deformed, and undeformed CFT, respectively) and a corresponding braneworld gravity sector. In particular, the split into $T\bar T$-deformed CFT coupled to $T\bar T$-like deformed Liouville gravity directly extends the Liouville description of the integrated Weyl anomaly into the bulk. The effective action is then used to perform an island-rule calculation for a non-asymptotic brane in an AdS/bCFT set-up, finding exact agreement with the Ryu-Takayanagi result. This extends previous holographic checks on the island rule to non-asymptotic regimes of application, as well as provides a Wald entropy interpretation for $T\bar T$ entanglement. Finally, we relate our effective theory and its higher derivative corrections to AdS$_3$ gravity with a fluctuating radial cutoff by explicit calculation of the on-shell action.

一句话导读：从径向哈密顿流导出EOW膜的有效作用，识别为类$T\bar T$形变CFT，并用于验证非渐近膜下的island规则与RT公式一致。

相关度：low — 论文属于全息对偶与量子纠缠方向，与用户重点关注的凝聚态、探针膜、QNM等支线无直接重叠。

绝缘体/超导体转变中的全息子区域复杂度

Holographic subregion complexity in insulator/superconductor transition

Yu Shi, Chikun Ding, Yuebing Zhou, Weike Deng, Sheng Long · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了在AdS-soliton背景中，完全反作用的绝缘体/超导体转变中的全息子区域复杂度(HSC)，并将其与全息纠缠熵(HEE)和基于复杂度=体积(CV)提议的全息复杂度进行比较。HSC和HEE都标志着二级相变。对于条带子系统，竞争性的连通和非连通Ryu-Takayanagi曲面产生了禁闭/退禁闭转变。在超导相中固定化学势时，HSC在临界宽度处出现有限跳跃，而HEE保持连续。超过该宽度，HSC随条带宽度线性增长，而HEE恒定。固定条带宽度时，对于ℓ<ℓ_c，HSC随化学势先减小后增大，与HEE相反；而对于ℓ>ℓ_c，HSC单调增大。在一致归一化并减去各自绝缘体参考后，半空间HSC和CV复杂度密度解析地相同。这些结果表明，HSC可以诊断绝缘体/超导体转变，但其定性响应仍对子系统尺度和纠缠楔拓扑敏感。

English: We study holographic subregion complexity (HSC) across a fully backreacted insulator/superconductor transition in an AdS-soliton background and compare it with holographic entanglement entropy (HEE) and holographic complexity based on the complexity=volume (CV) proposal. Both HSC and HEE signal the second-order transition. For a strip subsystem, competing connected and disconnected Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces give rise to a confinement/deconfinement transition. At fixed chemical potential in the superconducting phase, HSC exhibits a finite jump at the critical width, whereas HEE remains continuous. Beyond this width, HSC grows linearly with the strip width, while HEE is constant. At fixed strip width, HSC first decreases and then increases with chemical potential for $\ell<\ell_c$, opposite to HEE, but increases monotonically for $\ell>\ell_c$. After consistent normalization and subtraction of the respective insulating references, the half-space HSC and CV complexity densities are analytically identical. These results show that HSC can diagnose the insulator/superconductor transition, but its qualitative response remains sensitive to the subsystem scale and entanglement-wedge topology.

一句话导读：研究AdS-soliton背景中绝缘体/超导体转变的全息子区域复杂度，发现其可诊断相变但受子系统尺度和纠缠楔拓扑影响。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超导相变中的子区域复杂度，与用户关注的全息超流超导和量子信息量高度相关。

多方虫洞

Polytropic wormholes

Remo Garattini, Emmanuel N. Saridakis, Athanasios G. Tzikas · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：广义相对论中的可穿越虫洞需要非标准物质源，因此识别具有物理动机的状态方程尤为重要。我们研究了由多方状态方程支持的虫洞，在统一框架下考虑了均匀和非均匀构型。我们推导了相应解，并分析了多方参数对几何和能量条件的影响。在均匀情形下，多方构造给出了一个自洽的虫洞内部，其几何和物质含量由恒定的多方参数控制。在非均匀情形下，我们得到了一个一般解析表达式，表明几何完全由径向多方系数 ω(r) 决定。对于正的 ω(r)，物理上有意义的解的要求自然地将多方指数限制为奇数整数值。利用幂律轮廓，我们构造了具有不同参数区间和有限径向支撑的显式解类。有趣的是，对于指数 α=2γ−3，一种广义的“荒谬温和”可穿越虫洞构型自然出现。尽管张口条件意味着喉部处零能量条件违背，但非均匀框架允许控制其径向分布。我们的结果建立了多方物质与虫洞几何之间的系统联系，为构造具有局部化奇异物质的紧凑虫洞提供了一个灵活框架。

English: Traversable wormholes in general relativity require non-standard matter sources, making the identification of physically motivated equations of state particularly important. We investigate wormholes supported by a polytropic equation of state, considering homogeneous and inhomogeneous configurations within a unified framework. We derive the corresponding solutions and analyze the effects of the polytropic parameters on the geometry and energy conditions. In the homogeneous case, the polytropic construction yields a consistent wormhole interior whose geometry and matter content are governed by the constant polytropic parameters. For the inhomogeneous case, we obtain a general analytical expression showing that the geometry is completely determined by the radial polytropic coefficient $\omega(r)$. For positive $\omega(r)$, the requirement for physically meaningful solutions naturally restricts the polytropic exponent to odd integer values. Using a power-law profile, we construct explicit classes of solutions exhibiting distinct parameter regimes and finite radial support. Interestingly enough, for an exponent $\alpha=2\gamma-3$, a generalized absurdly benign traversable wormhole-like configuration emerges naturally. Although the flare-out condition implies null-energy-condition violation at the throat, the inhomogeneous framework allows its radial distribution to be controlled. Our results establish a systematic connection between polytropic matter and wormhole geometry, providing a flexible framework for constructing compact wormholes with localized exotic matter.

一句话导读：系统研究了多方状态方程支撑的均匀与非均匀可穿越虫洞，发现非均匀框架可控制奇异物质的径向分布。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究广义相对论中的经典可穿越虫洞解，属于经典引力支线，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”直接对应。

黑洞时空的准局部熵

A Quasi-local Entropy for Black Hole Space-times

Raymond Isichei · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们证明了对于静态球对称黑洞几何，有限半径边界上的 Brown-York 张量的角分量可以解释为 Tolman-Ehrenfest 温度与准局部熵的乘积。该熵在空间无穷远处为零，并随半径减小而单调增加，在视界处取得最大值，等于 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵。因此，该熵的行为为协变熵猜想提供了明确的验证。

English: We show that for static & spherically symmetric black hole geometries, the angular components of the Brown-York tensor on a finite radius boundary can be interpreted as the product of the Tolman-Ehrenfest temperature and a quasi-local entropy. This entropy vanishes at spatial infinity and monotonically increases as radius decreases, taking its maximum value on the horizon where it is equal to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. Thus, the behaviour of this entropy provides explicit verification of the covariant entropy conjecture.

一句话导读：提出黑洞时空的准局部熵定义，并验证其满足协变熵猜想。

相关度：high — 论文聚焦于经典引力中的黑洞时空准局部熵，与用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论直接相关。

三维中的加速度与旋转

Acceleration and Rotation in Three Dimensions

Cameron R D Bunney, Ruth Gregory, Robert B Mann · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们提出一族新的精确解，代表三维爱因斯坦引力中加速、旋转的点粒子和黑洞解。我们研究了它们的几何性质以及在三维anti-de Sitter空间中的整体嵌入。我们的结果扩展了三维爱因斯坦引力精确解目录，并为黑洞热力学、渐近对称性和全息的研究提供了有用的设置。

English: We present a new family of exact solutions representing accelerating, rotating, point particle and black hole solutions in three-dimensional Einstein gravity. We investigate their geometric properties and the global embedding in three dimensional anti-de Sitter space. Our results extend the catalogue of exact solutions in three-dimensional Einstein gravity and provide a useful setting for the study of black hole thermodynamics, asymptotic symmetries, and holography.

Bianchi I 时空中的截断稳定零收敛与方向刚性

Cutoff-Stable Null Convergence and Directional Rigidity in Bianchi I Spacetimes

Ye Zhou, Alan Zhang · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们推导了一个无端点光学刚性定理，并利用它在不假设空间度规在固定基下为对角形式的情况下，区分 Bianchi I 时空中的两种刚性机制。对于光滑、正则、无扭曲的零测地线汇，仿射 Raychaudhuri 方程将有限的零 Ricci 积分表示为膨胀边界项减去非负光学体项。如果过去和未来截断被独立移除，并且其两端下极限非负，则双侧完备性迫使光学张量和零 Ricci 收缩逐点消失。在 Bianchi I 中，这使空间度规在守恒余向量 的固定核上冻结。我们对所得饱和几何进行分类：非静态度规具有零条、一条或两条无向饱和线，等价于零条、两条或四条有向射线，而第三条线迫使静态性。在更强的逐点零收敛条件下，存在一条双侧完备的零测地线已经迫使空间度规恒定且宇宙时间区间为整个实数集；在笛卡尔万有覆盖上，时空为 Minkowski 空间。周期模型在较弱的截断稳定机制中达到四射线饱和界，在所有非饱和方向上 $I_p^{\mathrm{ind}}=-\infty$，而逐点零收敛在开放时间区间上失败。物质和类时平均零能量条件的推论在关于场方程和截断方案的显式匹配假设下给出。

English: We derive an endpoint-free optical rigidity theorem and use it to separate two rigidity regimes in Bianchi I spacetimes, without assuming that the spatial metric is diagonal in a fixed basis. For a smooth, regular, twist-free null congruence, the affine Raychaudhuri equation expresses a finite null-Ricci integral as an expansion boundary term minus a nonnegative optical bulk. If the past and future cutoffs are removed independently and their two-end liminf is nonnegative, two-sided completeness forces the optical tensor and the null Ricci contraction to vanish pointwise. In Bianchi I this freezes the spatial metric on the fixed kernel of the conserved covector. We classify the resulting saturation geometry: a non-static metric has zero, one, or two unoriented saturated lines, equivalently zero, two, or four oriented rays, and a third line forces staticity. Under the stronger pointwise null convergence condition, the existence of a single two-sided complete null geodesic already forces the spatial metric to be constant and the cosmic-time interval to be all of $\mathbb R$; on the Cartesian universal cover the spacetime is Minkowski. Periodic models attain the four-ray saturation bound in the weaker cutoff-stable regime, with $I_p^{\mathrm{ind}}=-\infty$ in every nonsaturated direction, while pointwise null convergence fails on open time intervals. Matter and achronal averaged-null-energy consequences are stated under an explicit matching assumption on field equations and cutoff prescriptions.

一句话导读：在 Bianchi I 时空中利用光学刚性定理区分两种零曲率刚性机制，并给出饱和几何分类。

相关度：low — 论文研究经典引力中的 Bianchi I 时空刚性，不涉及全息原理或用户指定的全息相关主题。

施瓦西黑洞的奇异旋量形变：扰动与稳定性

Exotic Spinor Deformation of a Schwarzschild Black Hole: Perturbations and Stability

Lu\'{i}s Rodolfo dos Santos Filho, Bertha Cuadros-Melgar · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们研究了奇异旋量拓扑如何修改施瓦西几何中费米子模式的传播。通过将拓扑梯度 ∂_μϕ 通过 tetrad 变形纳入 Dirac 算子，我们表明奇异旋量扇区自然诱导了施瓦西背景的有效几何形变。该构造的核心结果是出现了一个位于 r_topo=1/q 的拓扑阻抗面，它抑制了奇异旋量模式的径向群速度，产生了双折射传播模式。利用旋量扰动，我们讨论了灰体因子和准正则响应。准正则谱显示了背景度规的稳定性和超对称伴随势的非等谱性。

English: In this work we investigate how exotic spinorial topology modifies the propagation of fermionic modes in the Schwarzschild geometry. By incorporating a topological gradient, $\partial_\mu\varphi$, into the Dirac operator through a tetrad deformation, we show that the exotic spinorial sector naturally induces an effective geometric deformation of the Schwarzschild background. The central consequence of this construction is the emergence of a topological impedance surface at $\rtopo=1/q$, which suppresses the radial group velocity of the exotic spinorial modes, yielding a birefringent propagation pattern. Using a spinorial perturbation we discuss the greybody factor and the quasinormal response. The quasinormal spectrum shows the stability of the background metric and the absence of isospectrality for the superpartner potentials.

一句话导读：通过 tetrad 变形引入奇异旋量拓扑，研究对施瓦西黑洞费米子扰动的影响，发现拓扑阻抗面并分析 QNM 稳定性。

相关度：high — 论文聚焦于经典引力下黑洞的旋量扰动与准正则模，与用户重点标签中的“QNM与非线性演化”和“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关。

在耦合Euler-Heisenberg电动力学的f(R,T)引力中带磁荷黑洞的周期轨道

Periodic orbits around a magnetically charged black hole in $f(R,T)$ gravity coupled with Euler-Heisenberg electrodynamics

Jun-Long Huang, Chen-Kai Qiao, Jun Tao · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们系统研究了小致密天体绕f(R,T)引力中耦合Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学的磁荷黑洞运动的zoom-whirl周期轨道。我们评估了磁荷Q_m和Euler-Heisenberg参数a对最内层稳定圆轨道（ISCO）和边际束缚轨道（MBO）特性的影响。特别地，我们关注进动参数q，并系统研究磁荷及其他参数如何影响周期轨道轨迹。利用数值kludge方法，我们生成了这些周期轨道的引力波形。我们的研究结果表明，磁荷不仅能显著改变进动参数，还能改变周期轨道的轨道轨迹。此外，改变磁荷Q_m会导致引力波形的明显相移，而其他参数对引力波的影响相对较弱。

English: We systematically investigate the zoom-whirl periodic orbits of a small compact object orbiting a magnetically charged black hole in $f(R, T)$ gravity coupled with Euler-Heisenberg nonlinear electrodynamics. We evaluate the impact of the magnetic charge $Q_m$ and the Euler-Heisenberg parameter $a$ on the characteristics of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the marginally bound orbit (MBO). Particularly, we focus on the precession parameter q and systematically examine how the magnetic charge and other parameters affect the trajectories of periodic orbits. Using the numerical kludge method, we generate gravitational waveforms for these periodic orbits. The results presented in our work demonstrate that the magnetic charge can significantly modify not only the precession parameter but also the orbital trajectories of the periodic orbits. Moreover, changing the magnetic charge $Q_m$ could cause a significant phase shift in the gravitational waveforms, while other parameters exert a relatively weaker influence on the gravitational waves.

一句话导读：研究f(R,T)引力与Euler-Heisenberg电动力学下磁荷黑洞的周期轨道及其引力波形，发现磁荷显著影响进动和波形相移。

四阶后牛顿阶椭圆轨道的守恒量与基本频率：对齐自旋贡献

Constants of motion and fundamental frequencies for elliptic orbits at fourth post-Newtonian order: aligned-spin contributions

David Trestini, Jan Steinhoff · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：对于具有对齐自旋的偏心系统，我们计算了在第四后牛顿阶，守恒量（能量和角动量）与基本（径向和方位角）频率之间规范不变保守映射的所有自旋贡献。这扩展了之前无自旋映射，包括了自旋的线性、二次、三次和四次项以及相关的自旋可变形参数。我们还获得了派生量，如红移和陀螺不变量，以及圆轨道联系。我们注意到，决定最内稳定圆轨道无量纲频率的 Blanchet-Iyer-Favata 后牛顿稳定性判据实际上与圆轨道的近心点进动密切相关。最后，对于非束缚轨道，我们用所有自旋贡献补全了4PN散射角。

English: For eccentric systems with aligned spins, we compute all spin contributions to the gauge-invariant conservative map between the constants of motion (energy and angular momentum) and the fundamental (radial and azimuthal) frequencies at the fourth post-Newtonian order. This extends the nonspinning map derived in [arXiv:2511.10735] to include terms that are linear, quadratic, cubic, and quartic in spin, as well as the associated spin-deformability parameters. We also obtain derived quantities such as the redshift and gyroscopic invariants, as well as circular links. We notice that the Blanchet-Iyer-Favata post-Newtonian stability criterion that determines the dimensionless frequency of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) is in fact intricately related to the periastron advance for circular orbits. Finally, in the case of unbound orbits, we complete the 4PN scattering angle with all spin contributions.

一句话导读：该论文计算了椭圆轨道在四阶后牛顿阶下所有对齐自旋对守恒量与基本频率映射的贡献，并给出了相关派生量。

具有Λ的光子火箭的渐近引力辐射：点源、弦源和片源

Asymptotic Gravitational Radiation of Photon Rockets with $\Lambda$: Point, String, and Sheet Sources

Francisco Fern\'andez-\'Alvarez, Jos\'e M. M. Senovilla · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究具有一个类空Killing矢量和任意符号宇宙学常数Λ的纯辐射Robinson-Trautman度规在无穷远处引力辐射的存在性。这些度规描述了点、弦和片源的“光子火箭”，它们在时空中运动并发出零辐射。对于Λ≠0，我们使用基于渐近超坡印廷矢量的引力辐射存在性判据。对于点源，我们将已知的Λ=0结果扩展到Λ≠0，即Kinnersley火箭是唯一没有引力辐射的火箭。然而，对于弦源和片源，出现了没有引力辐射的新可能性。对于Λ>0，我们证明典型渐近超坡印廷矢量（关于scri的单位法向计算）的消失精确地表征了无辐射。另一方面，对于Λ<0，我们证明适当的判据是渐近超坡印廷矢量与scri相切的任意单位类时矢量相关的垂直于scri的分量的消失，或者等价地，Cotton-York张量与scri处全息应力张量的正比关系。特别地，我们提供了度规的显式例子，其中Cotton-York张量和全息应力张量交换，但由于两个张量不成比例，引力辐射仍然存在。我们还确定了scri处重标度Weyl张量的主零方向（PNDs）对Λ的两个符号，并将其相对于scri法向的几何与引力辐射的张量判据联系起来。

English: We study the existence of gravitational radiation at infinity for pure-radiation Robinson-Trautman metrics with one spacelike Killing vector and a cosmological constant $\Lambda$ of arbitrary sign. These metrics describe "photon rockets" with point, string, and sheet sources moving through spacetime while emitting null radiation. For $\Lambda\neq 0$, we use criteria for the existence of gravitational radiation based on the asymptotic super-Poynting vector. For point sources, we extend to $\Lambda\neq 0$ the known $\Lambda=0$ result that the Kinnersley rocket is the only rocket without gravitational radiation. However, for string and sheet sources, new possibilities without gravitational radiation arise. For $\Lambda>0$, we show that the vanishing of the canonical asymptotic super-Poynting vector---computed with respect to the unit normal to scri---exactly characterizes the absence of radiation. On the other hand, for $\Lambda<0$, we show that the appropriate criterion is the vanishing of the components normal to scri of the asymptotic super-Poynting vectors associated with any unit timelike vector tangent to scri or, equivalently, the proportionality between the Cotton-York tensor and the holographic stress tensor at scri. In particular, we provide explicit examples of metrics for which the Cotton-York and holographic stress tensors commute, yet gravitational radiation is present because the two tensors are not proportional. We also determine the principal null directions (PNDs) of the rescaled Weyl tensor at scri for both signs of $\Lambda$ and relate their geometry relative to the normal to scri to the tensorial criteria for gravitational radiation.

一句话导读：研究了带宇宙学常数的光子火箭度规在无穷远处的引力辐射，发现弦源和片源存在无辐射的新情形，并给出了基于Cotton-York张量与全息应力张量的判据。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究光子火箭的经典引力辐射，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，同时涉及全息应力张量，与用户重点标签直接相关。

全息斯特林发动机与通向卡诺效率之路

Holographic Stirling engines and the route to Carnot efficiency

Nikesh Lilani, Manus R. Visser · hep-th, cond-mat.quant-gas, gr-qc, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们计算了可逆斯特林循环（有无回热）的效率，工作物质涵盖范德瓦尔斯流体、量子理想气体（玻色和费米）、玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体、热共形场论（CFT）以及全息CFT。回热作为一种内部热量回收机制，通过减少与外部热库的净热交换来提高效率。对于带回热的斯特林循环，我们识别出一个实现卡诺效率的通用充分条件：在固定守恒电荷下，定容热容不依赖于体积。这保证了沿等容线在每一温度下，冷却时释放的热量等于加热时吸收的热量。因此，理想回热器可以可逆地储存并返还这些热量，并回复到初始热力学状态而不改变能量或熵。虽然该条件对经典理想气体和范德瓦尔斯流体成立，但对量子理想气体和CFT工作物质则不成立。对于对偶于AdS-施瓦西和AdS-赖斯纳-诺德斯特伦黑洞的热CFT态，我们得到了斯特林效率的精确表达式。在固定势系综中，带回热和无回热效率在大势极限下都趋于卡诺值。回热减小了与卡诺值的领先偏差，尽管两种效率的修正具有相同的渐近阶。

English: We compute the efficiency of the reversible Stirling engine, with and without regeneration, for a broad class of working substances including Van der Waals fluids, quantum ideal gases (Bose and Fermi), Bose-Einstein condensates, thermal conformal field theories (CFTs), and holographic CFTs. Regeneration acts as an internal heat recycling mechanism that enhances efficiency by reducing the net heat exchange with external reservoirs. For regenerative Stirling cycles, we identify a general sufficient condition for attaining Carnot efficiency: at fixed conserved charges, the fixed-volume heat capacity is independent of volume. This ensures that, at each temperature along the isochores, the heat released during cooling equals the heat absorbed during heating. An ideal regenerator can therefore store and return this heat reversibly, returning to its initial thermodynamic state with no net change in energy or entropy. While this condition is satisfied for classical ideal gases and Van der Waals fluids, it is violated for quantum ideal gases and CFT working substances. For thermal CFT states dual to AdS-Schwarzschild and AdS-Reissner-Nordstrom black holes, we obtain exact expressions for the Stirling efficiency. In the fixed-potential ensemble, both the regenerative and non-regenerative efficiencies approach the Carnot value in the large-potential limit. Regeneration reduces the leading deviation from the Carnot value, although both efficiencies have corrections of the same asymptotic order.

一句话导读：利用全息对应计算可逆斯特林循环的效率，给出实现卡诺效率的充分条件，并得到对偶于AdS黑洞的热CFT的精确斯特林效率。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息热力学和AdS/CFT字典应用，与用户标注的冷原子、超导、相变等具体研究支线没有直接交集，但涉及全息CFT热力学与AdS黑洞背景，相关度为中等。

可约一阶约束、规范对称性与三维引力耦合拓扑物质场的自由度

Reducible first-class constraints, gauge symmetry, and the degrees of freedom of three-dimensional gravity coupled to topological matter

Omar Rodr\'iguez-Tzompantzi · hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们为三维时空中一个引力拓扑物质系统建立了系统的哈密顿形式化，将标量物质场和二阶形式规范场与一阶Hilbert-Palatini引力耦合。我们进行了Dirac-Bergmann分析，得到了完整的约束结构，将其分类为第一类和第二类约束，并计算了它们的泊松括号代数。此外，我们写出了完整正则变量集合上规范对称性的哈密顿生成元的显式表达式，其中包含精确数量的规范参数。对这些参数的映射表明，规范变换在壳上重现了时空微分同胚和局域庞加莱对称性。由此，耦合模型的对称结构在无边界柯西面上得以确立。我们的正则分析进一步表明，第一类约束满足三个可约性条件。我们将这些条件追溯到Ricci型离壳恒等式以及二阶形式场携带的规范参数的冗余性。在扩展相空间中，它们仅模第二类部分封闭。一旦考虑这些条件，模型就不携带局域自由度。约化相空间上的基本辛结构通过Dirac括号的显式计算得以固定。这些括号和哈密顿规范生成元都是在扩展相空间中获得的，没有预先消除任何正则变量。最后，我们证明在有边界曲面上，由可约参数构造的改进生成元坍缩为单个边界积分，且该积分恒为零。

English: We develop a systematic Hamiltonian formulation for a gravitating topological matter system in three-dimensional spacetime, coupling a scalar matter field and a two-form gauge field to first-order Hilbert--Palatini gravity. We perform the Dirac--Bergmann analysis, obtaining the full structure of the constraints, classifying them into first- and second-class ones, and computing their Poisson bracket algebra. Furthermore, we write down the explicit expression for the Hamiltonian generator of gauge symmetries on the full set of canonical variables, containing the exact number of gauge parameters. A mapping of these parameters then shows that the gauge transformations reproduce on-shell the spacetime diffeomorphism and local Poincar\'e symmetries. The symmetry structure of the coupled model is thereby established on a Cauchy surface without boundary. Our canonical analysis further shows that the first-class constraints obey three reducibility conditions. We trace them back to an off-shell identity of Ricci type and to the redundancy of the gauge parameters carried by the two-form field. In the extended phase-space they close only modulo the second-class sector. Once they are taken into account, the model carries no local degrees of freedom. The fundamental symplectic structure on the reduced phase-space is fixed by the explicit computation of the Dirac brackets. Both these and the Hamiltonian gauge generator are obtained in the extended phase-space, with no canonical variable eliminated beforehand. Finally, we show that on a surface with boundary the improved generator built from the reducible parameters collapses to a single boundary integral, which vanishes identically.

一句话导读：该文通过Dirac-Bergmann分析系统研究了三维引力耦合拓扑物质场的约束结构，证明其无局域自由度，并建立了规范生成元与边界积分的关系。

熵界与纠缠岛的非普适性

Entropy bound and the non-universality of entanglement islands

Naman Kumar · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：岛法则通过将相应的内部伙伴放入其纠缠楔中，为合适的辐射子系统再现了Page曲线，并避免了Almheiri–Marolf–Polchinski–Sully (AMPS) 的monogamy冲突。这一机制本质上依赖于区域。我们将一个共同的紧致半经典支撑定义为有限的半经典域，其中包含所选晚期霍金模式族的内部伙伴的代表，而每个辐射子系统只需重建自己的伙伴。我们问，这些分开的逐模式重建能否在固定的蒸发时期于这样一个共同支撑内表示。物理动机是，视界正常性——即要求对于一个下落观察者视界保持光滑，并且半经典有效场论在其上保持有效——是一个局域性质，而解决AMPS的岛条件取决于用于重建的辐射子系统。利用共同支撑的有界零实现以及量子Bousso界，我们证明一旦支撑进入hyperentropic区域，不存在正则的共同紧致半经典支撑可以实现所有相关的AMPS伙伴扇区。该论证完全在半经典区域内进行，其中AMPS设置有效，并且不需要将零曲面延伸到Planckian奇点。

English: The island prescription reproduces the Page curve and avoids the Almheiri--Marolf--Polchinski--Sully (AMPS) monogamy conflict for suitable radiation subsystems by placing the corresponding interior partners in their entanglement wedges. This mechanism is intrinsically region-dependent. We define a common compact semiclassical support as a finite semiclassical domain that contains representatives of the interior partners of a chosen family of late Hawking modes, while each radiation subsystem need reconstruct only its own partner. We ask whether these separate mode-by-mode reconstructions can be represented within such a common support at a fixed evaporation epoch. The physical motivation is that horizon normalcy---the requirement that the horizon remain smooth for an infalling observer and that semiclassical effective field theory remain valid across it---is a local property, whereas the island condition that resolves AMPS for a given late mode depends on the radiation subsystem used for reconstruction. Using a bounded null realization of the common support together with the quantum Bousso bound, we show that no regular common compact semiclassical support can realize all of the relevant AMPS partner sectors once the support enters the hyperentropic regime. The argument is formulated entirely within the semiclassical region in which the AMPS setup is valid and does not require extending the null surface to the Planckian singularity.

一句话导读：证明在熵界约束下，无法在一个共同紧致半经典支撑内同时实现所有AMPS伙伴扇区，说明纠缠岛机制具有非普适性。

Curvature-Assisted Dynamical Compactification in a Pre-Inflationary Higher-Dimensional Universe

Yusuke Yamada · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了一种暴涨前的动力学紧致化场景，其中高维膨胀宇宙通过曲率辅助的模场俘获演化为有效四维宇宙。为实现可计算的半经典实现，我们考虑了在时间依赖的开放FRW背景下的简单五维S^1紧致化。体量子场既产生稳定radion的晚期Casimir贡献，也产生与宇宙学演化相关的早期Kaluza-Klein热贡献。我们表明，负曲率可以维持类似跟踪解的radion演化，使radion在随后的四维暴涨阶段稀释曲率残余之前被俘获在紧致真空中。虽然分析是在5D玩具模型中进行的，但它展示了一种更广泛的机制，即动力学紧致化可以在暴涨前的高维宇宙学中出现。

English: We investigate a pre-inflationary dynamical compactification scenario in which a higher-dimensional expanding universe evolves into an effectively four-dimensional one through curvature-assisted modulus trapping. To obtain a calculable semiclassical realization, we consider a simple five-dimensional $S^1$ compactification in a time-dependent open FRW background. Bulk quantum fields generate both the late-time Casimir contribution that stabilizes the radion and the early-time Kaluza-Klein thermal contributions relevant for the cosmological evolution. We show that negative curvature can sustain tracker-like radion evolution, allowing the radion to be trapped in a compactified vacuum before a subsequent four-dimensional inflationary phase dilutes the curvature remnant. While our analysis is performed in a 5D toy model, it illustrates a broader mechanism by which dynamical compactification can arise in a pre-inflationary higher-dimensional cosmology.

AdS黑洞的扩展哈密顿热力学与Carroll收缩

Extended Hamiltonian thermodynamics and Carroll contractions of AdS black holes

Yingnan Xu, Shuangshuang Chu · hep-th · v4 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们在时间生成元与牛顿耦合常数的关联收缩下研究扩展反德西特黑洞热力学。我们将收缩参数与物理单位联系起来，并区分平衡黑洞族内的变化与沿收缩方向的变化。从磁Carroll哈密顿作用量出发，我们推导了含可变宇宙学常数的边界恒等式，并评估了Schwarzschild–反德西特几何的能量、视界和压力贡献。我们验证了空间方程与时移方程，在余标架表述中保留boost联络。一个三形式势为宇宙学常数提供了共轭正则变量。我们将这种静态边界力学与Lorentz热力学收缩进行比较，后者的功项共同标度选择一个有限Carroll分支。在该分支上，温度消失而熵发散，而它们的共轭乘积保持有限。压力和体积的标度依赖于所选的物理归一化。带电荷、旋转及更高维族提供了进一步的热力学检验，包括旋转情形的渐近参考系和完整热力学体积。我们使用符号恒等式和数值微分来验证热力学变化、分解共轭贡献并检查其标度行为。这些构造将扩展的静态Carroll边界力学与Lorentz热力学收缩联系起来，并识别它们对应所需的相空间条件。

English: We study extended anti-de Sitter black-hole thermodynamics under correlated contractions of the time generator and Newton coupling. We relate the contraction parameter to physical units and distinguish variations within an equilibrium black-hole family from changes along the contraction. Starting from the magnetic Carroll Hamiltonian action, we derive the boundary identity with a variable cosmological constant and evaluate its energy, horizon and pressure contributions for Schwarzschild--anti-de Sitter geometries. We verify the spatial and lapse equations, retaining the boost connection in the coframe formulation. A three-form potential supplies a canonical variable conjugate to the cosmological constant. We compare this static boundary mechanics with Lorentzian thermodynamic contractions, whose common scaling of the work terms selects a finite Carroll branch. On this branch, temperature vanishes and entropy diverges while their conjugate product remains finite. The pressure and volume scalings depend on the chosen physical normalization. Charged, rotating and higher-dimensional families provide further thermodynamic tests, including the asymptotic frame and full thermodynamic volume for rotation. We use symbolic identities and numerical differentiation to verify the thermodynamic variations, resolve the conjugate contributions and check their scaling behaviour. These constructions relate extended static Carroll boundary mechanics to Lorentzian thermodynamic contractions and identify the phase-space conditions under which they correspond.

一句话导读：通过Carroll收缩研究AdS黑洞的扩展哈密顿热力学，建立静态边界力学与Lorentz热力学收缩的对应，并解析共轭体积、压力等贡献。

N=2 SYK模型BPS子空间中的混沌-可积转变

Chaos-Integrability Transition in the BPS Subspace of the $\mathcal{N}=2$ SYK Model

Leon Miyahara, Shono Shibuya · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了一个特定超对称模型的BPS子空间中的混沌-可积转变，该模型在混沌的N=2 SYK模型与可积的N=2“交换”SYK模型之间插值。利用BPS混沌框架，我们分析了投影到BPS子空间上的一个算符的谱。数值上我们发现，其谱统计在SYK极限附近表现出随机矩阵行为，并平滑过渡到可积极限附近的泊松统计。我们的结果提供了一个仅从BPS态诊断的混沌-可积交叉的直接例子。

English: We study chaos-integrability transition purely within a BPS subspace of a specific supersymmetric model that interpolates between the chaotic $\mathcal{N}=2$ SYK model and an integrable $\mathcal{N}=2$ "commuting" SYK model. Using the framework of BPS chaos, we analyze the spectrum of an operator projected onto the BPS subspace. We numerically find that its spectral statistics exhibit random-matrix behavior near the SYK limit and smoothly transitions to Poisson statistics near the integrable limit. Our results provide a direct example of a chaos-integrability crossover diagnosed solely from BPS states.

一句话导读：通过在N=2 SYK模型中插值并聚焦BPS子空间，数值展示了从随机矩阵到泊松统计的混沌-可积转变。

连续（非可逆）对称性的平直规范与紧致玻色子的非紧 BF SymTFT

Flat Gauging of Continuous (Non-invertible) Symmetries and Non-compact BF SymTFT for Compact Boson

Qiang Jia, Yi Zhang · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们通过对平直规范场构型求和来研究连续对称性的平直规范。文中聚焦二维紧致玻色子，构造了一般平直 U(1)_M×U(1)_W 背景下的环面配分函数。研究表明，平直规范 U(1)_M 或 U(1)_W 会使理论退紧致为非紧自由玻色子，且由于混合反常，对偶的 Z 背景与剩余的 U(1) 背景结合为非紧 R 对称背景。还重新讨论了自对偶半径：对角线 SO(3) ⊂ (SU(2)_L×SU(2)_R)/Z_2 的平直规范给出连续轨道折叠，超出通常 c=1 模空间。在轨道折叠分支上，研究了有限和连续非可逆平直规范，并解释连续情形需要零测度固定轨迹的处方。最后，将环面 sigma 模型的 SymTFT 表述为非紧 BF 理论，其拓扑边界态编码 Narain 模空间和 O(D,D;Z) T-对偶作用。

English: We study flat gauging of continuous symmetries by summing over flat gauge-field configurations. We focus on the two-dimensional compact boson and construct the torus partition function with general flat $U(1)_M\times U(1)_W$ backgrounds. We show that flat gauging either $U(1)_M$ or $U(1)_W$ decompactifies the theory to the non-compact free boson, and that the dual $\mathbb{Z}$ background combines with the remaining $U(1)$ background into a non-compact $\mathbb{R}$ symmetry background due to the mixed anomaly. We also revisit the self-dual radius, where flat gauging the diagonal $SO(3)\subset (SU(2)_L\times SU(2)_R)/\mathbb{Z}_2$, first pointed out by Gaberdiel and Suchanek, gives the continuous orbifold which lies outside the usual $c=1$ moduli space. On the orbifold branch, we study finite and continuous non-invertible flat gaugings and explain why the continuous case requires a prescription for zero-measure fixed loci on the moduli space. Finally, we formulate the SymTFT of torus sigma models as a non-compact BF theory, whose topological boundary states encode the Narain moduli space and the $O(D,D;\mathbb{Z})$ T-duality action.

一句话导读：通过非紧 BF SymTFT 研究二维紧致玻色子的平直规范、连续非可逆对称性与 Narain 模空间/T-对偶。

相关度：low — 论文属于广义对称性/SymTFT 的形式理论，与用户关注的超流超导、经典引力、冷原子、探针膜、QNM 等支线无直接交叉。

从 Fredholm 行列式到 AdS/CFT 可观测量：一个普适的强耦合框架

From Fredholm Determinants to AdS/CFT Observables: A Universal Strong-Coupling Framework

Bercel Boldis, Dennis le Plat · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了 N=4 超对称 Yang-Mills 理论中尖角反常维度以及 O(6) sigma 模型动力学质量隙在强 't Hooft 耦合下的 trans-series 结构。我们考虑尖角反常维度的一参数形变，即倾斜尖角，并回顾了通过其矩阵 Bessel 核 Fredholm 行列式表示获得的强耦合展开。该形变的优势在于非微扰尺度自然分离，揭示了在物理极限中隐藏的结构。受此启发，我们引入倾斜质量隙，它在形变参数的特定值处约化为物理 O(6) 质量隙，并与倾斜尖角有深刻联系。这一表述使我们能够确定 O(6) 质量隙的完整强耦合 trans-series 和 resurgence 结构，同时扩展并证明了连续非微扰修正之间的猜想关系。基于底层 Fredholm 行列式表示，我们构建了一个 Alien 微积分，直接从微扰展开生成这些 AdS/CFT 观测量的所有非微扰扇区。最后，我们推导了 O(6) 质量隙强耦合 trans-series 与平面 N=4 超对称 Yang-Mills 理论尖角反常维度之间的一个精确全阶关系。

English: We investigate the trans-series structure of the cusp anomalous dimension of $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory and the dynamically generated mass gap of the $\mathrm{O}(6)$ sigma model at strong 't Hooft coupling. We consider the one-parameter deformation of the cusp anomalous dimension, known as the tilted cusp, and review its strong-coupling expansion obtained through its representation in terms of Fredholm determinants with a matrix Bessel kernel. The advantage of this deformation is that the non-perturbative scales separate naturally, revealing structures that remain hidden in the physical limit. Motivated by this observation, we introduce the tilted mass gap, which reduces to the physical $\mathrm{O}(6)$ mass gap at a special value of the deformation parameter and exhibits a deep connection with the tilted cusp. This formulation allows us to determine the complete strong-coupling trans-series and resurgence structure of the $\mathrm{O}(6)$ mass gap, while extending and proving conjectured relations between successive non-perturbative corrections. Building on the underlying Fredholm determinant representation, we construct an Alien calculus that generates all non-perturbative sectors of these AdS/CFT observables directly from their perturbative expansions. Finally, we derive an exact all-orders relation between the strong-coupling trans-series of the $\mathrm{O}(6)$ mass gap and the cusp anomalous dimension of planar $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory.

一句话导读：利用 Fredholm 行列式和 Alien 微积分，系统地确定了 O(6) 质量隙和 N=4 SYM 尖角反常维度的强耦合 trans-series 结构，并建立精确全阶关系。

相关度：medium — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 可观测量的非微扰强耦合计算，使用 Fredholm 行列式与 resurgence 技术，直接相关于全息字典，但不直接对应用户重点标签中的任何具体分支。

从经典与量子混沌到全息

Classical and Quantum Chaos from Foundations to Holography

Bhasker Shukla · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：这些讲义在六讲中发展混沌诊断，从经典定义一直到全息，基于2026年在金奈数学研究所ST4工作坊的讲座。不要求预先了解混沌理论。第一讲和第二讲用几何方式定义经典混沌，并构建测量混沌的仪器，每个仪器都读取相空间轨迹。量子化移除了这一轨迹，第三讲和第四讲则转而从谱和本征态重建仪器：能级统计、随机矩阵理论、谱形状因子和本征态热化，这些既作为多体物理中热化的常用工具箱，也用于全息。它们返回的是分类和计数，而非速率。第五讲从平方对易子中恢复速率，将加扰视为算子增长，跟随对易子得到离时有序关联子（OTOC），标记该诊断何时不再可靠，并达到蝴蝶速度和混沌界限。第六讲构造了永恒黑洞、热场双体和该界限背后的冲击波几何，然后跟踪两个探针，它们的指数能够区分水平线结果无法区分的背景：一个追踪热力学相变，另一个分辨由背景场引起的各向异性。最后一部分是研究而非综述，文中的每个图都是用随源码提供的脚本计算出来的，而不是从文献中重绘。每讲都以练习结束。Claude Opus 5，Anthropic开发的AI助手，在作者指导下担任研究助理。

English: These notes develop the diagnostics of chaos from their classical definitions through to holography, in six lectures given at the ST4 Workshop, Chennai Mathematical Institute, in 2026. No prior exposure to chaos theory is assumed. Lectures 1 and 2 define classical chaos geometrically and build the instruments that measure it, every one of which reads a phase-space trajectory. Quantization removes that trajectory, and Lectures 3 and 4 rebuild the instruments from spectra and eigenstates instead: level statistics, random matrix theory, the spectral form factor and eigenstate thermalization, as much the working toolkit for thermalization in many-body physics as for holography. What they return is a classification and a count rather than a rate. Lecture 5 recovers the rate from the squared commutator, treats scrambling as operator growth, follows the commutator to the out-of-time-order correlator, marks where that diagnostic stops being reliable, and reaches the butterfly velocity and the chaos bound. Lecture 6 constructs the eternal black hole, the thermofield double and the shock-wave geometry behind that bound, then follows two probes whose exponents discriminate between backgrounds where the horizon result cannot, one tracking a thermodynamic phase transition, the other resolving an anisotropy induced by a background field. That last part is research rather than review, and every figure throughout is computed from the scripts supplied with the source rather than redrawn from the literature. Every lecture closes with exercises. Claude Opus 5, an AI assistant developed by Anthropic, worked as a research assistant under the author's direction.

一句话导读：六讲讲义系统阐述从经典混沌到全息混沌的诊断方法，包括OTOC、谱形状因子、蝴蝶速度、混沌界限，以及区分背景场的新探针研究。

相关度：medium — 本文以混沌诊断为主线，涉及全息混沌、OTOC和热化，与用户关注的QNM/非线性演化及全息混沌相关，但不直接涉及超流超导、相变气泡等特定支线。

双重标度平面 N=4 SYM 与 Carroll 全息

Doubly-scaled planar $\mathcal{N} = 4$ SYM & Carroll Holography

Arjun Bagchi, Prateksh Dhivakar, Alok Laddha, Partha Paul · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：在他们开创性的论文中，Okuda 和 Penedones (OP) 提出了一个关于渐近平坦时空 (AFS) 全息的有趣建议。他们证明了 AdS_5 × S^5 中四点玻色子树级弦振幅的平直空间极限对偶于四维 N=4 超杨-米尔斯 (SYM) 理论中四点关联函数的特定双重标度极限。在本文中，我们展示了由此产生的边界关联函数可用于在未来零无穷远上定义四维 Carroll 共形场论 (CFT)。这是通过将 SYM 中的双重标度关联函数映射到 AFS 的共形边界——零无穷远来实现的。因此，在 N=4 SYM 的无限't Hooft 耦合极限中出现了 Carroll CFT。我们将 OP 分析扩展到更高点函数，并展示了平直空间振幅上的 Gram 条件如何对 SYM 关联函数的双重标度极限施加约束。然后，我们利用膨胀弦振幅的软因子分解定理，为双重标度极限下 N=4 SYM 的 (1/2)-BPS 扇区写出递推关系。最后，我们展示了这种双重标度极限的基本思想可以作为工具包，通过非引力有效场论的平直空间振幅的某些积分变换，构建一类 Carroll 关联函数，从而定义 Carroll CFT。

English: In their seminal paper, Okuda and Penedones (OP) put forward an intriguing proposal towards holography in asymptotically flat spacetimes (AFS). They showed that the flat space limit of 4 point bosonic tree-level string amplitude in AdS$_{5}\, \times\, S^{5}$ is dual to a specific double scaling limit of 4 point correlator in four dimensional (4d) $\mathcal{N} = 4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theory. In this paper, we show that the resulting boundary correlators can be used to define a 4d Carrollian conformal field theory (CFT) on future null infinity. This is done by mapping the set of doubly scaled correlators in SYM to null infinity, the conformal boundary of AFS. A Carroll CFT thus emerges in the infinite 't Hooft coupling limit of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ SYM. We extend the OP analysis to higher point functions and show how the Gram conditions on flat space amplitude put constraints on the double scaling limit of SYM correlators. We then use the soft factorization theorem for dilatonic string amplitude to write a recursion relation for the $({1}/{2})$-BPS sector of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ SYM in the double scaling limit. Finally, we show that the essential ideas underlying such a double scaling limit can be used as a tool-kit to build a class of Carrollian correlators, and hence define a Carrollian CFT, via certain integral transforms of flat space amplitudes of non-gravitational effective field theories.

一句话导读：通过双重标度极限和零无穷远映射，从 N=4 SYM 构造 Carroll CFT，并推广到高点和 BPS 扇区。

相关度：low — 该研究与用户给出的重点标签（如全息超流超导、相变气泡、QNM等）关联度低，主要属于平直时空/Carroll 全息方向。

畴壁主导时期的引力波

Gravitational Wave with Domain Wall Dominance

Sungwoo Hong, Sung Mook Lee, Qiuyue Liang · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：当离散对称性自发破缺时会产生畴壁，长寿命的畴壁可以主导宇宙的能量密度。我们探讨了早期宇宙中存在“畴壁主导”阶段并以畴壁衰变结束的可能性。在畴壁主导阶段，宇宙经历尺度因子的幂律加速膨胀，并表现出相关频率模式的超视界时间演化。我们证明这会在随机引力波背景上留下独特印记，包括其振幅与壁张力无关。我们的发现为评估与畴壁主导相关的引力波发射提供了全面框架，使长寿命畴壁产生的引力波与其他宇宙学来源可区分，对未来引力波观测台具有重要意义。

English: Domain walls (DWs) can be produced when a discrete symmetry is spontaneously broken, and long-lived DWs can dominate the energy density of the universe. In this work, we explore the possibility that a "domain wall dominant (DWD)" phase existed in the early universe and ended with DW decay. During the DWD phase, the universe undergoes a power-law accelerated expansion of the scale factor and exhibits temporal superhorizon evolution of the relevant frequency modes. We show that this can lead to distinct features imprinted on the stochastic gravitational wave (GW) background including the independence of its amplitude from the wall tension. Our findings provide a comprehensive framework for evaluating GW emission associated with DWD, leading to distinguishable long-lived DW-induced GWs from other cosmological sources, with significant implications for future GW observatories.

一句话导读：早期宇宙畴壁主导阶段会在随机引力波背景上留下独特印记，其振幅与壁张力无关，为未来引力波观测提供可区分的信号。

相关度：medium — 论文研究畴壁主导时期的引力波，属于宇宙学与经典引力，与用户重点标签中的“畴壁/孤子/涡旋/非均匀”相关，但不涉及全息或凝聚态方向。

Einstein 方程作为与量子参考系关联的几何编码

Einstein's equation as a geometric encoding of correlations with quantum reference frames

Eduardo O. Dias · gr-qc, hep-th, quant-ph · v5 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：广义相对论中事件间的时空间隔相对于物质参考系才具有操作意义，而在量子力学中事件自然伴随与参考系局域自由度的关联。受这种互补性启发，我们提出几何-信息等价假说（GIEH），据此度规场在几何上编码了系统与局域参考系（LRF）之间本会出现的局域关联。在理想 LRF 条件下，GIEH 无需假设 AdS 背景或底层 CFT 即可给出完整非线性 Einstein 方程。该框架进一步允许背景关联为宇宙学常数提供信息论起源，将它们的局域互信息密度与 de Sitter 空间每单位热力学体积的 Gibbons-Hawking 熵联系起来，并允许在非零宇宙学常数下存在 Minkowski 参考时空。

English: Spacetime intervals between events in general relativity acquire an operational meaning relative to material reference frames, whereas in quantum mechanics events are naturally accompanied by correlations with the local degrees of freedom of the frame. Motivated by this complementarity, we propose a geometry-information equivalence hypothesis (GIEH), according to which the metric field geometrically encodes local correlations that would otherwise arise between the system of interest and a local reference frame (LRF). Imposing ideal-LRF conditions, the GIEH yields the full nonlinear Einstein equation, without assuming an AdS background or an underlying CFT. The framework further allows background correlations to provide an informational origin for the cosmological constant, relates their local mutual-information density to the Gibbons--Hawking entropy per unit thermodynamic volume of de Sitter space, and permits a Minkowski reference spacetime even for a nonzero cosmological constant.

一句话导读：提出几何-信息等价假说，从量子参考系的局域关联导出完整非线性 Einstein 方程，并讨论宇宙学常数与 de Sitter 熵的信息论起源。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典 Einstein 方程的推导、宇宙学常数与 de Sitter 熵问题，属于用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”方向。

全息软墙模型中强子光-光散射振幅的发散

Divergences in the hadronic light-by-light amplitude of the holographic soft-wall model

Josef Leutgeb, Jonas Mager, Anton Rebhan · hep-ph, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们使用Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) 近似来揭示一大类全息软墙模型中的发散和非交换极限情形。我们表明，对于涉及Chern-Simons项的各种可观测量（如矢量-矢量-轴矢（VVA）关联函数或强子光-光散射张量），单共振贡献的无限和不收敛。在某些情形下（如VVA关联函数），这些发散可以追溯到非交换极限，并可以通过直接在5维框架中使用体到边界传播子来避免。然而，强子光-光散射张量在相应的格林函数的5维表述中也会发散，从而阻碍了Melnikov-Vainshtein短距离约束的正确实现，甚至导致对muon g-2的无限贡献。我们还讨论了能够解决这一问题的标准软墙模型的修正。

English: We use the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation to uncover divergences and instances of non-commuting limits in a large class of holographic soft-wall models. We show that the infinite sum over single resonance contributions for a variety of observables involving the Chern-Simons term, such as the vector-vector-axial (VVA) correlator or the hadronic light-by-light tensor, does not converge. These divergences can in some cases (such as the VVA correlator) be traced back to non-commuting limits and avoided by working directly in the 5-dimensional setup with bulk-to-boundary propagators. However, the hadronic light-by-light scattering tensor also diverges in the 5-dimensional formulation with corresponding Green functions, preventing a correct implementation of the Melnikov-Vainshtein short-distance constraint and even leading to an infinite contribution to the muon $g-2$. We also discuss modifications of the standard soft-wall model that are able to resolve this issue.

一句话导读：该论文指出全息软墙模型中强子光-光散射振幅存在发散，影响muon g-2计算，并提出可能的模型修正。

相关度：low — 论文属于全息QCD领域，但与用户重点标注的全息超流超导、p波序参量、畴壁孤子、相变成核、QNM等具体研究支线无直接对应关系。

D维aether带电黑洞与Einstein-aether理论M子类中的aether波

$D$-dimensional aether charged black hole and aether waves in M-subclass of Einstein-aether theory

Chikun Ding, Yuebing Zhou, Yu Shi, Xiangyun Fu · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们在Einstein-aether理论的M子类中，通过具有单位范数矢量场（aether场 u^μ）破坏Lorentz不变性，得到了精确的D维aether带电黑洞解和引力波极化。该aether场可以是类时的或类空的，并且aether电荷Q_ae具有非零最小值，这与电荷不同。aether电类势u_t是正则的，而aether磁类势u_r在视界处奇异。尽管Lorentz对称性被破坏，Smarr公式和黑洞热力学第一定律仍可通过Killing势的扩展方法精确构造。然而，我们发现aether电荷的概念在Einstein-aether理论的c_i子类中不存在。对于具有类时aether场的线性化M子类Einstein-aether理论，我们发现自旋-2模式（通常的引力波）的速度仍等于1。但只有一个极化γ_12可以传播，而另一个γ_11在D=4时由于Lorentz对称性破缺效应不能传播。自旋-1模式（横向aether波）的速度也等于1。第三种模式是纵向aether-度规模式，它随时间线性变化，并非c_i子类Einstein-aether理论中报道的自旋-0模式。

English: We obtain an exact $D$-dimensional aether charged black hole solution and gravitational wave polarizations in the M-subclass of the Einstein-aether theory with Lorentz invariance violated by an unit norm vector field---the aether field $u^\mu $. This aether field can be timelike or spacelike and, the aether charge $Q_{\ae}$ has a nonzero minimum value, which is different from the electric charge. The aether electric-like potential $u_t$ is regular, and the aether magnetic-like potential $u_r$ is singular at the horizons. Though the Lorentz symmetry is broken, the Smarr formula and the first law of black hole thermodynamics can be exactly constructed via the extended method of Killing potential. However, we find this conception of the aether charge doesn't exist in the $c_i$ subclass of the Einstein-aether theory. For the linearized M-subclass Einstein-aether theory with the timelike aether field, we find the speed of spin-2 modes (the usual gravitational wave) is still equal to 1. But there is only one polarization $\gamma_{12}$ that can propagate, while the other one, $\gamma_{11}$ cannot propagate for $D=4$ due to the effect of Lorentz symmetry breaking. The speed of spin-1 modes (the transverse aether wave) is also equal to 1. The third kind mode is the longitudinal aether-metric mode, which is linearly time dependent and not the spin-0 mode reported in the $c_i$ subclass Einstein-aether theory.

一句话导读：在Einstein-aether理论的M子类中构造了精确D维aether带电黑洞，并分析了线性化后的引力波极化模式。

相关度：low — 该研究属于修改引力理论中的经典黑洞解与引力波极化分析，与全息原理或AdS/CFT对应无直接关联。

Kalb-Ramond黑洞背景下的中微子振荡

Neutrino oscillations in a Kalb-Ramond black hole background

Yuxuan Shi, A. A. Ara\'ujo Filho, K. E. L. de Farias, V. B. Bezerra, Amilcar R. Queiroz · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：该分析研究了当中微子轨迹在由Kalb-Ramond场产生的黑洞周围展开时，自发洛伦兹对称性破缺如何重塑周围几何，进而影响中微子的行为。研究首先关注可观测后果：中微子-反中微子湮灭能量输出的改变、沿路径累积的振荡相位的移动，以及引力透镜引起的味道转换概率的畸变。随后将这些特征与支配中微子传播的洛伦兹破缺时空结构联系起来。数值模拟针对两味和三味描述进行，并考虑正常和倒转质量顺序。

English: The analysis examines how neutrinos behave when their trajectories unfold around a black hole sourced by a Kalb-Ramond field, where spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking reshapes the surrounding geometry. Instead of following the conventional order, the study focuses first on the observable consequences: alterations in the neutrino-antineutrino annihilation energy output, shifts in the oscillation phase accumulated along the path, and distortions in flavor conversion probabilities induced by gravitational lensing. These features are then tied to the Lorentz-violating spacetime structure, which governs the propagation of the neutrinos. Numerical simulations are carried out for both two- and three-flavor descriptions, with normal and inverted mass orderings.

一句话导读：在Kalb-Ramond黑洞背景下研究中微子振荡的引力透镜效应与洛伦兹破缺影响。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及经典黑洞背景下的中微子传播，与全息或量子引力主线关联较弱，但属于经典引力/广义相对论领域。

de Sitter时空中线性化引力波的一致截断

Consistent Truncation of Linearized gravitational waves in de Sitter space-time

Ghanashyam Date, Harsh · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：将来自有界源的引力波观测与源附近的局部动力学和环境联系起来，是利用有界源进行引力波观测的重要一步。为了分离和识别源的不同特征，辐射场和源分布的多极展开至关重要。在实际层面，这需要截断到某个多极阶，由此产生一致性问题。在[CHK]中指出了在某种渐近形式中出现不允许的log(r)项，并提出了与源守恒一致的特定四极截断。在[NKD80]中注意到截断解虽然满足波动方程，但可能无法满足规范条件。在本工作中，我们澄清了一致性问题，并纠正了[NKD80]中的几个错误陈述。通过显式地对共形时间积分规范条件和源守恒方程来分析一致性。这给出了一致截断的一般步骤。该步骤在共形坐标系的广义谐和规范以及Bondi坐标系的Bondi规范中得到了验证。

English: An important step in using observations of gravitational waves from bounded sources is to relate the observed waveforms far away from a source to the local dynamics and environment of the source. To isolate and identify different features of the source, multipole expansion of radiation field as well as that of the source distribution is crucial. At a practical level, this requires truncation to some multipole order to be employed and a consistency issue arises. This was flagged in \cite{CHK} as emergence of disallowed $\log(r)$ terms in certain asymptotic forms and a particular quadrupolar truncation was proposed which was consistent with the source conservation. In \cite{NKD80}, it was noticed that a truncated solution while satisfying the wave equation, may fail to satisfy the gauge condition. In this work, we clarify the consistency issue and also correct a couple of erroneous statements in \cite{NKD80}. The consistency is analyzed by explicitly integrating the gauge conditions and the source conservation equations over the conformal time. This gives a general procedure for a consistent truncation. The procedure is verified in generalized harmonic gauge in conformal chart and also in the Bondi gauge in Bondi chart.

一句话导读：澄清了de Sitter时空中线性化引力波多极截断的一致性问题，并给出了通用截断程序。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究de Sitter时空中的经典引力波及其截断，属于经典引力/广义相对论研究支线。

反常二维Thomas-Whitehead引力的约束分析与量子化

Constraint Analysis and Quantization of Anomalous 2-D Thomas-Whitehead Gravity

Eric Biedke, Salvatore Quaid, Vincent Rodgers · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：二维有效Polyakov作用量通常表现为弦理论和费米子耦合二维引力的反常贡献。然而，由于重新参数化不变性，即使在动态光锥和度规的ADM形式等不同规范下，有效作用量也会产生消失的哈密顿量作为约束。另一方面，二维引力理论自然地作为Virasoro代数余伴随轨道上的几何作用量出现。Thomas-Whitehead引力形式扩展了有效Polyakov作用量，使得定义轨道的余伴随元素成为一个动力学场，即微分同胚场。本文通过充分理解的二维引力反常贡献的量子化，考察微分同胚场的作用。首先在动态光锥中完成，然后采用度规的ADM形式。为了研究动态微分同胚场，我们在闵可夫斯基背景下重复约束分析，其中微分同胚场的动力学源于Thomas-Whitehead作用量。给微分同胚场添加动力学似乎消除了消失的哈密顿量；然而，用P张量表示微分同胚场恢复了哈密顿约束。可以将此研究与规范Wess-Zumino-Witten作用量进行类比，其中规范场通过加入杨-米尔斯项而变得动力学。

English: The two-dimensional effective Polyakov action is often realized as the anomalous contributions of string theories and fermions coupled to gravity in two dimensions. However, as a result of the reparametrization invariance, one finds that the effective action produces vanishing Hamiltonians as constraints even in disparate gauges such as the dynamical light-cone and the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) formalism of the metric. On the other hand, two-dimensional gravitational theories naturally arise as geometric actions on the coadjoint orbits of the Virasoro algebra. The Thomas-Whitehead gravity formalism extends the effective Polyakov action in such a way that the defining coadjoint element for the orbit becomes a dynamical field, viz. the diffeomorphism field. In this work, we examine the role the diffeomorphism field plays through the well-understood quantization of the two-dimensional anomalous contributions to gravity. This is first done in the dynamical light-cone and then with the ADM formalism of the metric. To examine a dynamical diffeomorphism field, the constraint analysis is then repeated in a Minkowski background, where the dynamics of the diffeomorphism field arise from the Thomas-Whitehead action. Adding dynamics to the diffeomorphism field appears to remove the vanishing Hamiltonians; however, expressing the diffeomorphism in terms of the P-tensor recovers the Hamiltonian constraint. One can compare this investigation to that of a gauge Wess-Zumino-Witten action where the gauge field has become dynamical through the inclusion of a Yang-Mills term.

一句话导读：本文分析了二维Thomas-Whitehead引力中动态微分同胚场的约束结构，发现其动力学可消除消失哈密顿量但通过P张量恢复约束，并对比了WZW模型。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦于二维反常引力的约束分析与量子化，而非全息原理、AdS/CFT或用户指定的凝聚态/QCD/热化等方向，仅与经典引力理论有一定关联。

Dymnikova 黑洞潮汐力

Dymnikova Black Hole Tidal Forces

M. H. Mac\^edo, A. A. M. Silva, R. R. Landim · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：本文研究了 Dymnikova 正则黑洞的潮汐性质及其对径向自由下落大质量粒子的影响。从 Dymnikova 静态球对称解出发，推导了类时径向测地线方程，并构建了适用于自由下落观测者的正交标架。随后得到了潮汐张量的径向和角向分量，并分析了它们对黑洞质量和德西特核心特征长度尺度的依赖关系。在远距离处，潮汐力恢复为 Schwarzschild 行为；而在正则中心附近，两个分量均保持有限，反映了时空的非奇异性。我们表明，径向和角向潮汐力在事件视界内的特征半径处消失并改变符号，表明拉伸与压缩区域之间的转变。从事件视界外静止释放的粒子会到达 Cauchy 视界内的转向点，而非正则中心。我们还对两组初始条件求解了测地偏离方程，并考察了偏离矢量径向和横向分量的演化。尽管解渐近地复现 Schwarzschild 行为，但在内部区域存在显著差异：偏离矢量分量在转向点前保持有限，而 Schwarzschild 情形中相应的径向分量在奇点处发散。这些结果表明德西特核心如何正则化延伸下落体的潮汐动力学。

English: In this work we investigate the tidal properties of the Dymnikova regular black hole and their ef fects on massive particles in radial free-fall. Starting from Dymnikova static, spherically symmetric solution, we derive the equations governing timelike radial geodesics and construct an orthonormal tetrad adapted to a free-falling observer. We then obtain the radial and angular components of the tidal tensor and analyze their dependence on the black hole mass and the characteristic length scale of the de Sitter core. At large radial distances, tidal forces recover Schwarzschild behavior, whereas near the regular center, both components remain finite, reflecting the non-singular nature of the spacetime. We show that the radial and angular tidal forces vanish and change sign at characteris tic radii inside the event horizon, indicating transitions between stretching and compression regimes. A particle released from rest outside the event horizon reaches a turnaround point located inside the Cauchy horizon, rather than reaching the regular center. We also solve the geodesic deviation equations for two sets of initial conditions and examine the evolution of the radial and transverse components of the deviation vector. Although the solutions asymptotically reproduce Schwarzschild behavior, they differ significantly in the inner region: the deviation vector components remain finite up to the turnaround point, whereas the corresponding radial component in the Schwarzschild case diverges at the singularity. These results demonstrate how the de Sitter core regularizes the tidal dynamics of extended falling bodies.

一句话导读：研究Dymnikova正则黑洞的潮汐力，发现其内部有限且在特征半径处改变符号，正则核心消除了Schwarzschild奇点处的发散。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力中的正则黑洞潮汐效应研究，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关，但与全息等主要支线关联较弱。

经典引力/广义相对论

非多项式引力中的有理正则黑洞

Rational regular black holes in non-polynomial gravity

Aimeric Coll\'{e}aux · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们考虑 d≥4 的非多项式纯引力理论，其球对称二维约化在极小尺度上对广义相对论的修改极小。这些理论满足 Birkhoff 定理，并在任意数量的耦合常数下允许精确黑洞解。这些解具有熵的对数级前导修正和牛顿势的量子化修正，两者都源于正则化的临界阶 d=2p 理论，在偶数维度中该理论与二维轨道空间的欧拉示性数相关。这些黑洞可以无需无穷多修正塔即可变得正则，使用任意阶 p>d/2 的不变量。在偶数维度中，其光滑性随修正数量线性变化且无需微调。我们构造了一类理论，其唯一的（无质量）真空是 (A)dS₄ 或 M₄ 的极端黑洞变形，熵为零，因此能斯特热力学第三定律在熵中无间断地满足。我们证明麦克斯韦场不会破坏度规的正则性，而作用量中的简单非最小耦合能够正则化电场。考虑无穷修正塔，我们构造具有单个视界的准正则黑洞，以及对于任意质量都具有近极端内视界的正则 (A)dS 核心黑洞。在带电时，这两种类型的黑洞能够防止或抑制质量暴涨，只要质量和电荷满足形如 M > αℓ + βQ²/ℓ 的不等式，其中 α 和 β 是取决于具体模型的数值因子，ℓ 是控制高能修正的长度尺度。准正则黑洞足以解决库仑奇点问题，无需非最小耦合。

English: We consider the $d\geq 4$ non-polynomial pure gravity theories defined so that their 2D reduction to spherical symmetry minimally modifies that of General Relativity at small distances. They satisfy Birkhoff theorem and admit exact black hole solutions for an arbitrary number of couplings. These solutions have a logarithmic leading correction to the entropy and a quantum-like correction to the Newtonian potential, both arising from the regularised critical order $d=2p$ theory, which is related in even dimensions to the Euler characteristic of the 2D orbit space. These black holes can be made regular, without the need for infinite towers of corrections, using invariants of any order $p>d/2$. In even dimensions, their smoothness is linear in the number of corrections and does not require fine-tuning. We construct a class of theories admitting as unique (massless) vacuum an extremal black hole deformation of (A)dS$_4$ or M$_4$ with vanishing entropy, so that Nernst's third law of thermodynamics is satisfied without discontinuity in the entropy. We show that a Maxwell field does not disrupt the regularity of the metric, and that simple non-minimal couplings in the action enable to regularise the electric field. Considering infinite towers of corrections, we construct quasi-regular black holes with one horizon as well as regular (A)dS-core ones with a near-extremal inner horizon for any mass. When charged, these two types of black holes prevent or tame the mass inflation provided the mass and charge satisfy an inequality of the form $M > \alpha \ell + \beta Q^2 / \ell$, where $\alpha$ and $\beta$ are numerical factors depending on the specific model, while $\ell$ is the length scale controlling the high-energy corrections. The quasi-regular black holes are sufficient to resolve the Coulomb singularity without the need for non-minimal couplings.

一句话导读：提出一类非多项式纯引力理论中的精确黑洞解，这些解可正则化并满足能斯特第三定律，且不依赖无限高阶修正。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与黑洞解的研究，与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线相关，但未涉及全息或量子引力内容，因此相关性中等。

Hayward黑洞在EFT修正光子传播下的引力透镜效应

Gravitational Lensing of Hayward Black Holes with EFT-Corrected Photon Propagation

Takamasa Kanai · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：我们研究了当考虑有效场论(EFT)对光子传播的修正时，Hayward规则黑洞能否通过强引力透镜与Schwarzschild黑洞在观测上区分开来。我们推导了电磁场与时空曲率非最小耦合所导致的修正光子传播定律，并分析了修正后的光子在Hayward时空中的轨迹。利用强偏折极限，我们推导了光子球和偏转角对数发散部分的修正。尽管EFT修正参数上很小，但在临界传播区域附近其效应可能被增强，那里偏转角呈现对数发散。我们评估了代表Hayward参数值下的强偏折可观测量，并与Schwarzschild情形进行了比较。我们发现，EFT对光子传播的修正可以在强透镜观测上留下特征印记。此外，随着Hayward参数g^3接近其临界值，EFT修正的贡献变得更加显著，表明依赖曲率的光子传播修正可能在近临界区域变得特别重要。这些结果表明，强引力透镜可能为区分Hayward规则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞提供一种手段。

English: We investigate whether a Hayward regular black hole can be observationally distinguished from a Schwarzschild black hole through strong gravitational lensing when effective field theory (EFT) corrections to photon propagation are taken into account. We derive the modified photon propagation law induced by non-minimal couplings between the electromagnetic field and spacetime curvature, and analyze the resulting photon trajectories in the Hayward spacetime. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the corrections to the photon sphere and the logarithmically divergent part of the deflection angle. Although the EFT corrections are parametrically small, their effects can be enhanced near the critical propagation region, where the deflection angle exhibits a logarithmic divergence. We evaluate the strong-deflection observables for representative values of the Hayward parameter and compare them with the Schwarzschild case. We find that EFT corrections to photon propagation can leave characteristic imprints on strong-lensing observables. Furthermore, the contribution of the EFT corrections becomes more pronounced as the Hayward parameter $g^3$ approaches its critical value, indicating that curvature-dependent corrections to photon propagation can become particularly relevant in the near-critical regime. These results suggest that strong gravitational lensing may provide a means of distinguishing Hayward regular black holes from Schwarzschild black holes.

一句话导读：通过考虑EFT修正的光子传播和强引力透镜，发现Hayward规则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞在近临界区域可被观测区分。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的引力透镜研究，与用户重点标签中的经典引力支线相关，但不涉及全息或其他优先支线。

经典引力中的物质介导纠缠：束缚势与定域化的抑制

Matter-Mediated Entanglement in Classical Gravity: Suppression by Binding Potentials and Localization

Ziqian Tang, Chen Yang, Hanyu Xue, Haochen Yu, Zizhao Han, Zikuan Kan, Yulong Liu · quant-ph · v3 · 更新 2026-09-01

中文摘要：引力诱导纠缠（GIE）被广泛视为非经典引力的关键证据。然而，最近有工作认为经典引力背景可以通过分离质量之间的虚物质传播产生纠缠。这里我们表明，一旦考虑真实的物质动力学，这种机制是可以忽略的。对于束缚或凝聚态物质，束缚势将所谓的远程物质介导相互作用转化为指数级短程作用，对于代表性的束缚能，其衰减长度仅为几皮米——远小于原子尺度，因此在实验相关距离上可以忽略。对于非束缚物质，在显著的波包重叠之前该通道仍然可以忽略，而重叠之后波包不再代表两个空间上分离的子系统。因此，一旦物理定域化和有限时间物质动力学被一致地处理，所提出的经典物质介导机制并不会挑战通常将GIE解释为非经典引力的证据的做法。

English: Gravity-induced entanglement (GIE) is widely regarded as key evidence of nonclassical gravity. Recent work, however, argues that a classical gravitational background can generate entanglement through virtual matter propagation between separated masses. Here we show that this mechanism is negligible once realistic matter dynamics is taken into account. For bound or condensed matter, the binding potential converts the claimed long-range matter-mediated interaction into an exponentially short-ranged one, with an attenuation length of only a few picometers for representative binding energies-well below atomic dimensions and therefore negligible under experimentally relevant distances. For unbound matter, the channel remains negligible before significant wave-packet overlap, while after overlap the packets no longer represent two spatially separated subsystems. Thus the proposed classical matter-mediated mechanism does not challenge the usual interpretation of GIE as evidence for nonclassical gravity once physical localization and finite-time matter dynamics are treated consistently.

一句话导读：表明经典引力通过物质传播产生纠缠的机制在真实束缚物质中会被指数抑制，因此不能推翻引力诱导纠缠作为量子引力证据的解读。

相关度：medium — 论文讨论了经典引力框架下物质介导纠缠的可行性，与经典引力/广义相对论支线相关；但与主要全息或凝聚态方向无直接交集。

AdS/CFT、超极限与婴儿宇宙

AdS/CFT, Ultralimits and Baby universes

Eyoab Bahiru · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-08-31

中文摘要：我们提出边界理论 CFT_p（其中 p ∈ βN/N），在边界时空维数大于一时，这些理论可被解释为 AdS/CFT 对偶语境下的理论系综。这些理论仅在边界 CFT 的无限 N 极限中涌现，并依赖于 p；p 对应于在存在混沌或振荡算子时，取广义无限 N 极限的不同方式。这种广义无限 N 极限被称为“超极限”（ultralimit），而 p 是自然数集上的自由超滤子（free ultrafilter）。我们提出引力路径积分在其每个扇区中计算 CFT_p 的平均值；在适当情况下，它会产生婴儿宇宙和时空虫洞。我们将此提议应用于 Antonini-Sasieta-Swingle (AS^2) 类态。

English: We propose boundary theories, CFT$_{p}$ with $p\in \beta\mathbb{N}/\mathbb{N}$, which can be interpreted as an ensemble of theories in the context of AdS/CFT duality, when the boundary spacetime dimension is more than one. These theories are only emergent in the infinite $N$ limit of the boundary CFT and depend on $p$, which corresponds to the different ways one can take a generalized form of the infinite $N$ limit, in the presence of a chaotic or an oscillatory operator. This generalized infinite $N$ limit is called an \emph{ultralimit}, and $p$ is a \emph{free ultrafilter} on $\mathbb{N}$. We propose that the gravitational path integral computes an average of CFT$_{p}$ in each of its sectors; and in the appropriate cases, it produces baby universes and spacetime wormholes. We apply this proposal to the Antonini-Sasieta-Swingle (AS$^{2}$) like states.

一句话导读：论文提出一种通过超极限构造的边界理论系综，解释 AdS/CFT 中的婴儿宇宙和时空虫洞。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦于 AdS/CFT 基础与量子引力中的婴儿宇宙/系综思想，与用户指定的凝聚态、QNM、相变等研究支线无直接关联。

引力冲击波碰撞中辐射的潮汐效应

Tidal effects on radiation in gravitational shockwave collisions

Isabelle Blackstad, Himanshu Raj, Raju Venugopalan · hep-th, gr-qc, hep-ph, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-31

中文摘要：我们计算了轻质紧凑天体（能量 μ_L）与重紧凑天体（黑洞）（能量 μ_H ≫ μ_L）冲击波碰撞中的高频引力波辐射。我们通过冲击波传播子获得了形式解，这些传播子提供了对 O(μ_L μ_H^n) 潮汐响应函数的快照，这些函数对黑洞形成的经典和潜在的半经典动力学敏感。在微扰展开中，O(μ_L μ_H) 的首个非平凡贡献是来自两个 Regge 引力子融合产生的 Lipatov 顶点的引力子发射。对于引力子频率 ω→0，Lipatov 振幅还原为单引力子交换 Weinberg 振幅的超相对论极限。在 O(μ_L μ_H^2) 阶，Lipatov 振幅包含一项解释引力子被 Regge 引力子再散射的部分，以及另一项对应从融合三个 Regge 引力子的顶点发射引力子的部分。这些非平凡贡献在软极限下同样简化为双引力子交换的 Weinberg 辐射振幅。我们在整篇文章中强调与 Yang-Mills 冲击波的稀密碰撞中胶子辐射的双复制结构。我们将冲击波结果与 Veneziano 等人的 eikonal 多重散射框架联系起来。

English: We compute high frequency gravitational wave radiation in shockwave collisions of a light mass compact object with energy $\mu_L$ off a heavy compact object (black hole) with energy $\mu_H \gg \mu_L$. A formal solution is obtained in terms of shockwave propagators that provide snapshots of $O(\mu_L\mu_H^n)$ tidal response functions sensitive to classical and potentially semi-classical dynamics of black hole formation. In a perturbative expansion, the first nontrivial contribution of $O(\mu_L\mu_H)$ is the emission of a graviton from the Lipatov vertex generated by the fusion of two reggeized gravitons. For graviton frequencies $\omega\rightarrow 0$, the Lipatov amplitude reduces to the ultrarelativistic limit of the single-graviton exchange Weinberg amplitude. At $O(\mu_L\mu_H^2)$, the Lipatov amplitude includes a piece accounting for the rescattering of the graviton off a reggeized graviton and another, corresponding to graviton emission from a vertex fusing three reggeized gravitons. These nontrivial contributions again simplify to the Weinberg radiative amplitude for two-graviton exchange in the soft limit. We highlight throughout double copy structures to gluon radiation in dilute-dense collisions of Yang-Mills shockwaves. We relate our shockwave results to the eikonal multiple scattering framework of Veneziano et al.

一句话导读：该论文通过冲击波传播子和 Regge 方法计算了致密天体碰撞中的高频引力波辐射，揭示了在软极限下与 Weinberg 振幅的联系，并强调了双复制结构。

相关度：medium — 论文研究引力冲击波碰撞中的引力波辐射，属于经典引力/广义相对论领域，与用户关注的经典引力支线相关，但未涉及全息对偶。
